########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_duoface_diagnostic_deb.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Package a separate read-only DuoFace SpringBoard diagnostic for Dopamine rootless."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, pathlib, shutil, subprocess, tempfile

PACKAGE="com.duoface.diagnostic"
VERSION="0.1.0~diag1"
ARCH="iphoneos-arm64"
DEST=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
FILTER=pathlib.Path("DuoFaceDiagnostic.plist")
PREVIOUS=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12-speed8compact/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12+speed8compact_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PREVIOUS_SHA="2fe8892c5ef8762e1e83b0fd6c4a029e25fc11758cc98fdbbb851bf6637e68ad"

def sha(p: pathlib.Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def run(*args: object) -> None:
    subprocess.run([str(v) for v in args],check=True)

def main() -> None:
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--dylib",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument("--output",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument("--report",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    opts=p.parse_args()
    assert PREVIOUS.is_file() and sha(PREVIOUS)==PREVIOUS_SHA, "Speed8 baseline must be preserved"
    assert opts.dylib.is_file() and opts.dylib.stat().st_size>1000
    assert FILTER.is_file() and FILTER.read_text().strip()==(
        '{ Filter = { Executables = ("SpringBoard"); }; }')
    assert not opts.output.exists() and not opts.report.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-diagnostic-") as temp:
        root=pathlib.Path(temp)/"pkg"
        target=root/DEST
        target.mkdir(parents=True)
        dylib=target/"DuoFaceDiagnostic.dylib"
        plist=target/"DuoFaceDiagnostic.plist"
        shutil.copyfile(opts.dylib,dylib)
        dylib.chmod(0o755)
        shutil.copyfile(FILTER,plist)
        run("ldid","-S",dylib)
        run("lipo",dylib,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        run("plutil","-lint",plist)
        control=root/"DEBIAN"/"control"
        control.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        control.write_text(
            f"Package: {PACKAGE}\n"
            "Name: DuoFace Diagnostic Log\n"
            f"Version: {VERSION}\n"
            f"Architecture: {ARCH}\n"
            "Maintainer: DuoFace Project\n"
            "Section: Tweaks\n"
            "Priority: optional\n"
            "Depends: com.duoface.independent\n"
            "Description: Independent read-only CarPlay state logger for DuoFace; no change to DuoFace itself.\n"
        )
        relative=[p.relative_to(root).as_posix() for p in root.rglob("*")
                  if p.is_file() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"]
        assert sorted(relative)==sorted([str(DEST/"DuoFaceDiagnostic.dylib"),
                                         str(DEST/"DuoFaceDiagnostic.plist")])
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("".join(
            hashlib.md5((root/x).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+x+"\n"
            for x in sorted(relative)))
        opts.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",root,opts.output)
    def field(key: str) -> str:
        return subprocess.check_output(
            ["dpkg-deb","-f",str(opts.output),key],text=True).strip()
    assert (field("Package"),field("Version"),field("Architecture"))==(PACKAGE,VERSION,ARCH)
    assert field("Depends")=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert sha(PREVIOUS)==PREVIOUS_SHA, "No changes to existing DuoFace archive"
    info={
        "status":"BUILT_STATIC_QA_DEVICE_UNTESTED",
        "package":PACKAGE,"version":VERSION,"architecture":ARCH,
        "depends":"com.duoface.independent",
        "dylib_only_springboard":True,
        "no_replacement_of_duoface_files":True,
        "file_count":len(relative),
        "files":sorted(relative),
        "speed8_baseline_sha256_unchanged":PREVIOUS_SHA,
        "output_sha256":sha(opts.output),
        "runtime_log":"/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceDiagnostic-SpringBoard.jsonl",
        "uikit_events_observed_not_carplay_icon_tap_hook":True,
        "has_method_hooks":False,"creates_uiwindow":False,
        "sileo_published":False,"device_tested":False
    }
    opts.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    opts.report.write_text(json.dumps(info,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(info))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_duoface_diagnostic_deb.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_duoface_diagnostic2_deb.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""DIAG2 replaces only the separate diagnostic package, never com.duoface.independent."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
PACKAGE="com.duoface.diagnostic"
VERSION="0.2.0~diag2"
ARCH="iphoneos-arm64"
ROOT=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
PLIST=pathlib.Path("DuoFaceDiagnostic2.plist")
SPEED8=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12-speed8compact/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12+speed8compact_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="2fe8892c5ef8762e1e83b0fd6c4a029e25fc11758cc98fdbbb851bf6637e68ad"
def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def run(*args):subprocess.run([str(x) for x in args],check=True)
def main():
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    for x in ("dylib","output","report"):a.add_argument("--"+x,required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    args=a.parse_args()
    assert SPEED8.is_file() and sha(SPEED8)==PIN,"Speed8 baseline modified/missing"
    assert args.dylib.is_file() and args.dylib.stat().st_size>1000
    assert PLIST.read_text().strip()=='{ Filter = { Executables = ("SpringBoard", "CarPlayApp", "CarPlay", "DashBoard"); }; }'
    assert not args.output.exists() and not args.report.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-diag2-") as t:
        pkg=pathlib.Path(t)/"pkg"
        target=pkg/ROOT
        target.mkdir(parents=True)
        lib=target/"DuoFaceDiagnostic2.dylib"
        dst=target/"DuoFaceDiagnostic2.plist"
        shutil.copyfile(args.dylib,lib)
        shutil.copyfile(PLIST,dst)
        lib.chmod(0o755)
        run("ldid","-S",lib)
        run("lipo",lib,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        run("plutil","-lint",dst)
        files=sorted(p.relative_to(pkg).as_posix() for p in target.iterdir() if p.is_file())
        assert files==sorted([str(ROOT/"DuoFaceDiagnostic2.dylib"),str(ROOT/"DuoFaceDiagnostic2.plist")])
        debian=pkg/"DEBIAN"
        debian.mkdir()
        (debian/"control").write_text(
            f"Package: {PACKAGE}\nName: DuoFace Diagnostic DIAG2\nVersion: {VERSION}\nArchitecture: {ARCH}\n"
            "Maintainer: DuoFace Project\nSection: Tweaks\nPriority: optional\n"
            "Depends: com.duoface.independent\n"
            "Description: Optional DuoFace CarPlay-Dashboard/SpringBoard trace for iPhone X; ABI-guarded and no UI changes.\n"
        )
        (debian/"md5sums").write_text("".join(hashlib.md5((pkg/x).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+x+"\n" for x in files))
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",pkg,args.output)
    def field(s):return subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.output),s],text=True).strip()
    assert tuple(field(s) for s in ("Package","Version","Architecture","Depends"))==(PACKAGE,VERSION,ARCH,"com.duoface.independent")
    assert sha(SPEED8)==PIN
    report={
       "status":"BUILT_STATIC_QA_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
       "package":PACKAGE,"version":VERSION,"architecture":ARCH,
       "replaces_diag1_package_on_install":True,
       "depends":"com.duoface.independent",
       "file_count":len(files),"files":files,
       "output_sha256":sha(args.output),
       "speed8_baseline_sha256_unchanged":PIN,
       "signature_guarded_message_hooks":True,
       "no_duoface_independent_files":True,
       "creates_uiwindow":False,
       "forces_activation":False,
       "log_files":["/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceDiagnostic2-SpringBoard.jsonl","/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceDiagnostic2-CarPlay.jsonl"],
       "carplay_executable_names_are_candidates_only":True,
       "sileo_published":False,"device_tested":False
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_duoface_diagnostic2_deb.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_duoface_diagnostic3_deb.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""DIAG3 replaces only the separate diagnostic package, never com.duoface.independent."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
PACKAGE="com.duoface.diagnostic"
VERSION="0.3.0~diag3"
ARCH="iphoneos-arm64"
ROOT=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
PLIST=pathlib.Path("DuoFaceDiagnostic3.plist")
SPEED8=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12-speed8compact/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12+speed8compact_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="2fe8892c5ef8762e1e83b0fd6c4a029e25fc11758cc98fdbbb851bf6637e68ad"
def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def run(*args):subprocess.run([str(x) for x in args],check=True)
def main():
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    for x in ("dylib","output","report"):a.add_argument("--"+x,required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    args=a.parse_args()
    assert SPEED8.is_file() and sha(SPEED8)==PIN,"Speed8 baseline modified/missing"
    assert args.dylib.is_file() and args.dylib.stat().st_size>1000
    assert PLIST.read_text().strip()=='{ Filter = { Executables = ("SpringBoard", "CarPlayApp", "CarPlay", "DashBoard"); }; }'
    assert not args.output.exists() and not args.report.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-diag3-") as t:
        pkg=pathlib.Path(t)/"pkg"
        target=pkg/ROOT
        target.mkdir(parents=True)
        lib=target/"DuoFaceDiagnostic3.dylib"
        dst=target/"DuoFaceDiagnostic3.plist"
        shutil.copyfile(args.dylib,lib)
        shutil.copyfile(PLIST,dst)
        lib.chmod(0o755)
        run("ldid","-S",lib)
        run("lipo",lib,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        run("plutil","-lint",dst)
        files=sorted(p.relative_to(pkg).as_posix() for p in target.iterdir() if p.is_file())
        assert files==sorted([str(ROOT/"DuoFaceDiagnostic3.dylib"),str(ROOT/"DuoFaceDiagnostic3.plist")])
        debian=pkg/"DEBIAN"
        debian.mkdir()
        (debian/"control").write_text(
            f"Package: {PACKAGE}\nName: DuoFace Diagnostic DIAG3\nVersion: {VERSION}\nArchitecture: {ARCH}\n"
            "Maintainer: DuoFace Project\nSection: Tweaks\nPriority: optional\n"
            "Depends: com.duoface.independent\n"
            "Description: Optional DuoFace CarPlay-Dashboard/SpringBoard trace for iPhone X; ABI-guarded and no UI changes.\n"
        )
        (debian/"md5sums").write_text("".join(hashlib.md5((pkg/x).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+x+"\n" for x in files))
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",pkg,args.output)
    def field(s):return subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.output),s],text=True).strip()
    assert tuple(field(s) for s in ("Package","Version","Architecture","Depends"))==(PACKAGE,VERSION,ARCH,"com.duoface.independent")
    assert sha(SPEED8)==PIN
    report={
       "status":"BUILT_STATIC_QA_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
       "package":PACKAGE,"version":VERSION,"architecture":ARCH,
       "replaces_diag1_package_on_install":True,
       "depends":"com.duoface.independent",
       "file_count":len(files),"files":files,
       "output_sha256":sha(args.output),
       "speed8_baseline_sha256_unchanged":PIN,
       "signature_guarded_message_hooks":True,
       "no_duoface_independent_files":True,
       "creates_uiwindow":False,
       "forces_activation":False,
       "log_files":["/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceDiagnostic3-SpringBoard.jsonl","/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceDiagnostic3-CarPlay.jsonl"],
       "carplay_executable_names_are_candidates_only":True,
       "sileo_published":False,"device_tested":False
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report))
if __name__=="__main__":main()

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native_trial.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Đóng DEB THỬ DuoFace nguyên ruột: giữ toàn bộ UI/host/visual/function gốc.

Không thay thế hay chép đè bản gốc trên thiết bị; installer từ chối khi
có package kế thừa được cài. Cơ chế test thử chưa phải kết quả runtime.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import plistlib
import shutil
import stat
import subprocess
import tempfile
import os
import sys

ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SRC=ROOT/"dist/candidates/menu-native1/com.duonavi.app_1.0.3-12_menu-native1_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
EXPECTED="ce971b42230d355421b2b1d99793a1f62ffb75008365ea74a3467d859cc1de3a"
MANIFEST=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/EXACT_COPY_AUDIT.json"
VERSION="0.1.0~native1"
ID="com.duoface.independent"
LIB="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries"
NATIVE="A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease"
NEW_FILES={f"{LIB}/{NATIVE}.dylib",f"{LIB}/{NATIVE}.plist"}

def hash_file(p:pathlib.Path)->str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def run(cmd:list[str])->None:
    subprocess.run(cmd,check=True)

def file_inventory(stage:pathlib.Path)->dict[str,str]:
    out={}
    for p in stage.rglob("*"):
        if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and not str(p.relative_to(stage)).startswith("DEBIAN/"):
            out[p.relative_to(stage).as_posix()]=hash_file(p)
    return out

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--policy",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--report",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    arg=parser.parse_args()
    assert SRC.exists() and hash_file(SRC)==EXPECTED, "Sai nguồn DuoFace gốc"
    assert arg.policy.is_file() and arg.policy.read_bytes()[:4] in (
        bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"),bytes.fromhex("cafebabe"),
        bytes.fromhex("bebafeca")), "Thiếu dylib ARM64 Mach-O cho bản thử"
    old=json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert old["source_sha256"]==EXPECTED
    wanted={k:v["sha256"] for k,v in old["all_staged_entries"].items() if v["kind"]=="file" and not k.startswith("DEBIAN/")}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native-test-") as d:
        stage=pathlib.Path(d)/"root"
        run(["dpkg-deb","-R",str(SRC),str(stage)])
        srcmap=file_inventory(stage)
        assert srcmap==wanted | {"var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.duonavi.sync.plist":
              hash_file(stage/"var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.duonavi.sync.plist")}, "Baseline payload khác bản đã được kiểm kê"
        (stage/"var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.duonavi.sync.plist").unlink()
        assert file_inventory(stage)==wanted, "Làm thay đổi UI/host gốc trước khi cài shim"

        # Original UI, Dashboard, geometry and full assets kept BYTE-FOR-BYTE
        # including CFBundleIdentifier com.duoface.duofaceui (host code depends on it).
        addon=stage/LIB/f"{NATIVE}.dylib"
        shutil.copyfile(arg.policy,addon)
        os.chmod(addon,0o755)
        filters={
            "Filter":{"Bundles":[
                "com.apple.springboard","com.apple.SpringBoard",
                "com.apple.CarPlay","com.apple.CarPlayApp","com.apple.dashboard",
                "com.duoface.duofaceui",
            ]}
        }
        plist=(stage/LIB/f"{NATIVE}.plist")
        plist.write_bytes(plistlib.dumps(filters,fmt=plistlib.FMT_XML))

        metadata=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        lines=metadata.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        replace={
            "Package":ID,"Version":VERSION,
            "Name":"DuoFace Mới (Native thử nghiệm)",
            "Description":"DuoFace UI va loi goc, thuc nghiem khong dong bo cap phep. Can thiet bi test va khong cai dong thoi goi cu.",
            "Maintainer":"DuoFace Independent",
        }
        has=set()
        result=[]
        for line in lines:
            key=line.split(":",1)[0] if ":" in line else ""
            if key in ("Conflicts","Replaces","Provides","Breaks","Pre-Depends"):
                continue
            if key in replace:
                result.append(f"{key}: {replace[key]}");has.add(key)
            else:result.append(line)
        for key,value in replace.items():
            if key not in has:result.append(f"{key}: {value}")
        metadata.write_text("\n".join(result)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")

        # Prevent overwriting paths belonging to existing legacy DuoFace/DuoNavi.
        # No dpkg Conflicts or automatic removal. User must consciously migrate.
        pre=stage/"DEBIAN/preinst"
        pre.write_text("""#!/bin/sh
set -eu
for legacy in com.duonavi.app com.duoface.app; do
    status="$(dpkg-query -W -f='${Status}' "$legacy" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    if [ "$status" = "install ok installed" ]; then
        echo "DuoFace Native TEST: goi cu $legacy dang cai. Tu choi ghi de. KHONG xoa goi cu." >&2
        exit 1
    fi
done
exit 0
""")
        post=stage/"DEBIAN/postinst"
        post.write_text("""#!/bin/sh
set -eu
if command -v uicache >/dev/null 2>&1; then uicache -a || true; fi
echo "DuoFace Native TEST installed. Restart SpringBoard/CarPlay before device testing."
exit 0
""")
        prem=stage/"DEBIAN/prerm"
        prem.write_text("#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n")
        for p in (pre,post,prem):os.chmod(p,0o755)

        # Regenerate every checksum after the staging transition.
        md5lines=[]
        for p in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and not p.relative_to(stage).parts[0]=="DEBIAN":
                md5lines.append(f"{hashlib.md5(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}  {p.relative_to(stage).as_posix()}")
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5lines)+"\n")
        os.chmod(stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums",0o644)
        checked=file_inventory(stage)
        assert set(checked)-set(wanted)==NEW_FILES
        assert {k:checked[k] for k in wanted}==wanted, "Payload không còn nguyên byte!"
        assert not any("sync.plist" in key for key in checked)
        assert plistlib.loads((stage/"var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/Info.plist").read_bytes())["CFBundleIdentifier"]=="com.duoface.duofaceui"
        assert plistlib.loads(plist.read_bytes())==filters
        arg.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run(["dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(stage),str(arg.out)])

    package_id=subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(arg.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()
    ver=subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(arg.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()
    assert (package_id,ver)==(ID,VERSION)
    report={
        "status":"ORIGINAL_UI_NATIVE_TRIAL_PACKAGE_BUILT_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
        "package":ID,"version":VERSION,
        "source_original_sha256":EXPECTED,
        "original_unchanged_payload_file_count":len(wanted),
        "original_unchanged_payload_sha256":wanted,
        "removed_files":["var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.duonavi.sync.plist"],
        "added_runtime_files":sorted(NEW_FILES),
        "license_checks":"native trial redirects Objective-C license entrypoints; original embedded code remains present, not proven unused",
        "side_by_side":False,
        "installer_refuses_legacy_installed":True,
        "legacy_package_automatically_removed":False,
        "original_app_info_bundle_id_preserved":True,
        "original_dashboard_binary_unchanged":True,
        "original_ui_source_rewritten":False,
        "original_postinst_reused":False,
        "device_tested":False,
        "sileo_published":False,
        "deb_sha256":hash_file(arg.out),"deb_size":arg.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    arg.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    arg.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k not in ("original_unchanged_payload_sha256",)},
                     ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native_trial.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native10_lifecycle_trial.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Isolated, explicitly user-authorized native10 device trial from tested native9.

WARNING: this is experimental. Objective-C constructor, singleton and ivar
cleanup changes can prevent CarPlay from loading. This build has no runtime
safety guarantee. The tested native9 DEB is immutable and available to restore.

Exactly 19 previously fingerprinted method bodies are modified. Eight
CarPlay-screen methods and every other native9 payload file must be retained.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from build_native5 import FILES, cmd, preserve_entitlements_and_resign, sha
from build_native8 import files_and_digests

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SOURCE=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native9/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native9_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
SOURCE_SHA="e4e1122a494d32af6ec42c97c4288f6effb6716fbb81cb52b81b746280d4d97e"
VERSION="0.1.0~native10"
PLAN=ROOT/"audit/payload-migration/NATIVE9_19_LIFECYCLE_INMEMORY_PATCH_PREVIEW.json"
NOP=bytes.fromhex("1f2003d5")

def build():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=Path)
    args=p.parse_args()
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes())==SOURCE_SHA
    assert not args.out.exists(),"Cannot overwrite any existing candidate"
    plan=json.loads(PLAN.read_text())
    assert plan["source_sha256"]==SOURCE_SHA
    assert len(plan["patch_rows"])==19
    assert len(plan["carplay_original_checksums"])==8
    assert plan["method_runtime_lifecycle_safe"] is False
    assert plan["proposed_binary_written"] is False
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SOURCE),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SOURCE),"Version"],text=True).strip()=="0.1.0~native9"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native10-") as td:
        tmp=Path(td)
        stage=tmp/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",SOURCE,stage)
        before=files_and_digests(stage)
        signatures={}
        patches=[]
        for binary,rel in FILES.items():
            target=stage/rel
            original=target.read_bytes()
            mutable=bytearray(original)
            intervals=[]
            for entry in [x for x in plan["patch_rows"] if x["binary"]==binary]:
                start=entry["offset"]
                end=start+entry["span_length"]
                prev=original[start:end]
                assert sha(prev)==entry["original_span_sha256"]
                new=bytes.fromhex(entry["preview_stub_prefix_hex"])+NOP*entry["preview_tail_arm64_nop_repeat"]
                assert len(new)==len(prev)
                assert sha(new)==entry["preview_span_sha256"]
                assert prev!=new
                for a,b in intervals: assert end<=a or start>=b
                intervals.append((start,end))
                mutable[start:end]=new
                patches.append({
                    "binary":binary,"class":entry["class"],"selector":entry["selector"],
                    "offset":start,"span_length":len(new),"before_sha256":sha(prev),
                    "after_sha256":sha(new),
                    "may_break_runtime":entry["release_blocker"]
                })
            for cp in (x for x in plan["carplay_original_checksums"] if x["binary"]==binary):
                a=cp["offset"];size=next(m["length"] for m in
                    [*json.loads((ROOT/"audit/payload-migration/NATIVE9_19_LIFECYCLE_HIDE_PLAN_READONLY.json").read_text())["by_binary"][binary]["carplay_byte_exact_keep"]]
                    if m["file_offset"]==a)
                assert sha(bytes(mutable[a:a+size]))==cp["sha256_original_still_exact"]
            assert len(mutable)==len(original)
            target.write_bytes(mutable)
            signatures[binary]=preserve_entitlements_and_resign(target,original,tmp,binary)
        assert len(patches)==19
        control=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        text=control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native9" in text
        control.write_text(text.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native9","Version: "+VERSION),encoding="utf-8")
        sums=[]
        for file in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if file.is_file() and not file.is_symlink() and file.relative_to(stage).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                sums.append(hashlib.md5(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+file.relative_to(stage).as_posix())
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(sums)+"\n")
        after=files_and_digests(stage)
        assert before.keys()==after.keys()
        modified=sorted(k for k in before if before[k]!=after[k])
        assert modified==sorted([*FILES.values(),"DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"]),modified
        assert before["var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib"]==after["var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib"]
        assert before["var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.dylib"]==after["var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.dylib"]
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",stage,args.out)
    report={
        "status":"NATIVE10_EXPERIMENTAL_ONLY_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
        "source_sha256":SOURCE_SHA,
        "version":VERSION,
        "package":"com.duoface.independent",
        "architecture":"iphoneos-arm64",
        "modified_method_count":len(patches),
        "carplay_method_byte_guard_count":8,
        "modified_files":modified,
        "unchanged_other_payload_files":len(before)-len(modified),
        "patches":patches,
        "preserved_signing":signatures,
        "native9_rollback_preserved":True,
        "runtime_safety_proven":False,
        "device_tested":False,
        "sileo_published":False,
        "deb_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "deb_size":args.out.stat().st_size
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!="patches"},indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__":
    build()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native10_lifecycle_trial.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native11_removed_license_strings.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Build native11 with 52 original license-only literal contents erased.

Real DEB artifact from a byte-pinned, user-tested native10; unlike the
prior in-memory audit this produces an actual new, separately versioned
rootless package and signs the two modified Mach-O binaries.

ONLY 52 exact audited literal byte ranges are erased to NUL in __cstring.
The 312 previously modified Objective-C method bodies, the eight CarPlay
routines, class/selector metadata and every other feature stay untouched.
Erasing strings is not the same as removing all license classes or methods.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

from build_native5 import FILES, cmd, preserve_entitlements_and_resign, sha
from build_native8 import files_and_digests
from remove_native11_license_literals import (
    SRC, EXPECTED, INVENTORY, PROTECTED,
    erase_binary, selected, CANDIDATE_TOTAL,
)

VERSION="0.1.0~native11"
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]

def make():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=Path)
    args=p.parse_args()
    assert sha(SRC.read_bytes())==EXPECTED,"User-tested native10 SHA mismatch"
    assert not args.out.exists(),"Never overwrite existing DEB"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SRC),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SRC),"Version"],text=True).strip()=="0.1.0~native10"
    audit_catalog=json.loads(INVENTORY.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    guard=json.loads(PROTECTED.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    baseline=json.loads((ROOT/"audit/payload-migration/NATIVE11_LICENSE_LITERAL_REMOVAL_IN_MEMORY_ONLY.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert baseline["count_physically_erased_literal_contents"]==CANDIDATE_TOTAL==52
    assert baseline["total_erased_original_nonzero_bytes"]==1816
    assert baseline["source_native10_sha256"]==EXPECTED
    assert baseline["staged_unsigned_binaries_written"] is False
    saved=[]
    summaries={}
    signed={}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native11-build-") as work:
        tmp=Path(work)
        stage=tmp/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",SRC,stage)
        before=files_and_digests(stage)
        for name,relative in FILES.items():
            file=stage/relative
            original=file.read_bytes()
            removed,rows,stats=erase_binary(original,name,audit_catalog,guard)
            assert len(original)==len(removed)
            assert stats["unsigned_erased_sha256"]==baseline["per_binary"][name]["unsigned_erased_sha256"]
            assert stats["erased_string_count"]==baseline["per_binary"][name]["erased_string_count"]
            assert stats["changed_nonzero_bytes"]==baseline["per_binary"][name]["changed_nonzero_bytes"]
            file.write_bytes(removed)
            signed[name]=preserve_entitlements_and_resign(file,original,tmp,name)
            modified=file.read_bytes()
            # Re-signing may alter __LINKEDIT / CodeDirectory but not user data.
            for row in rows:
                begin=row["offset"];end=begin+row["length"]
                assert modified[begin:end]==bytes(row["length"]),(name,begin)
            summaries[name]={
                **stats,
                "signed_binary_sha256":sha(modified),
                "signature_action":signed[name],
                "erased_ranges_in_signed_output_verified":True,
            }
            saved.extend(rows)
        assert len(saved)==CANDIDATE_TOTAL
        control=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        old=control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native10" in old
        control.write_text(old.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native10","Version: "+VERSION),encoding="utf-8")
        checks=[]
        for file in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if file.is_file() and not file.is_symlink() and file.relative_to(stage).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                checks.append(hashlib.md5(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+file.relative_to(stage).as_posix())
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(checks)+"\n")
        after=files_and_digests(stage)
        assert before.keys()==after.keys(),"No package entries may be removed/added"
        modified_paths=sorted(k for k in after if after[k]!=before[k])
        assert modified_paths==sorted([*FILES.values(),"DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"])
        for constant in (
            "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib",
            "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.dylib",
            "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.plist",
            "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.plist",
        ):
            assert before[constant]==after[constant],constant
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",stage,args.out)
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Architecture"],text=True).strip()=="iphoneos-arm64"
    report={
        "status":"NATIVE11_SIGNED_DEB_52_OLD_LICENSE_STRING_CONTENTS_PHYSICALLY_ERASED",
        "source_tested_native10_sha256":EXPECTED,
        "version":VERSION,"package":"com.duoface.independent","architecture":"iphoneos-arm64",
        "byte_erased_count":sum(r["length"] for r in saved),
        "erased_string_count":len(saved),
        "category_counts":{
            "localization_lookup_key_preserve":40,
            "diagnostic_log_preserve":12
        },
        "modified_existing_paths":modified_paths,
        "baseline_package_entry_count":len(before),
        "all_non_target_payload_files_byte_identical":True,
        "all_8_carplay_detection_method_bodies_unchanged":True,
        "all_312_previous_neutralized_method_bodies_unchanged":True,
        "all_visual_logo_split_fullscreen_payload_unchanged":True,
        "objc_class_and_selector_metadata_not_removed":True,
        "all_license_data_and_metadata_fully_removed":False,
        "removed_original_literal_content_not_binary_section_layout":True,
        "signed_modified_macho":True,
        "signed_binaries":summaries,
        "removed_rows":saved,
        "runtime_localization_or_indirect_callers_unverified":True,
        "user_device_test_for_new_package":False,
        "sileo_published":False,
        "tested_native10_rollback_preserved":True,
        "deb_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "deb_size":args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!="removed_rows"},indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=="__main__":
    make()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native11_removed_license_strings.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed1_candidate.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Native12-based, reproducible speed-bubble test DEB. Does not publish to Sileo."""
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
HASH="85d754ea887eb8dd0659f65a5b2e3425564f617983937579753bad1a69433bf7"
VER="0.1.0~native12+speed1"
DIR=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
SRC=("VietMapGoogleMapsSource","WazeGMapHUDSource","GofaGMapHUDSource")
ADD=("DuoFaceSpeedHUD","DuoFaceSpeedSources")
def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def run(*args):subprocess.run([str(x) for x in args],check=True)
def snap(root):
    return {f.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(f) for f in root.rglob("*")
        if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    for name in ("stage","gmapreport","hud","observer","output","report"):
        p.add_argument("--"+name,type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    a=p.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE)==HASH,"Incorrect Native12 base DEB"
    assert a.stage.is_dir() and not a.output.exists() and a.output!=BASE
    info=json.loads(a.gmapreport.read_text())
    assert info["original_native12_extracted"] and len(info["copied"])==8
    assert info["single_duoface_hud_only"]
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="df-native12-speed1-") as tmp:
        original=pathlib.Path(tmp)/"original"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,original)
        before=snap(original); staged=snap(a.stage)
        assert len(before)>30
        assert all(staged.get(k)==v for k,v in before.items()),"Native12 changed in stage"
        assert len(staged)-len(before)==8,"Not three GMap publishers + two licenses"
        assert not any(x in k for k in staged for x in
            ("GMapHUDOverspeedScreen","GMapHUDPlacementRelay","GoogleMapsVietMap"))
        for name in SRC:
            dll=a.stage/DIR/(name+".dylib")
            flt=a.stage/DIR/(name+".plist")
            assert dll.is_file() and flt.is_file()
            run("lipo",dll,"-verify_arch","arm64")
            run("ldid","-S",dll)
        mods={}
        for name,binary in zip(ADD,(a.hud,a.observer)):
            assert binary.is_file() and binary.stat().st_size>3000
            dll=a.stage/DIR/(name+".dylib")
            flt=a.stage/DIR/(name+".plist")
            assert not dll.exists() and not flt.exists()
            shutil.copy2(binary,dll);dll.chmod(0o755)
            shutil.copy2(pathlib.Path(name+".plist"),flt)
            run("ldid","-S",dll)
            run("lipo",dll,"-verify_arch","arm64")
            run("plutil","-lint",flt)
            mods[name]={"dylib_sha256":sha(dll),"filter_sha256":sha(flt)}
        ctrl=a.stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        text=ctrl.read_text()
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native12" in text
        ctrl.write_text(text.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native12","Version: "+VER,1))
        after=snap(a.stage)
        assert all(after.get(k)==v for k,v in before.items())
        protected=("DuoFaceDashboard","DuoFaceFullscreenBridge",
            "DuoFaceVisualHook","DuoFaceLogoGripOnly")
        for name in protected:
            path=str(DIR/(name+".dylib"))
            assert before[path]==after[path]
        assert "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI" in before
        md5=[]
        for path in sorted(after):
            checksum=hashlib.md5((a.stage/path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            md5.append(checksum+"  "+path)
        (a.stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5)+"\n")
        a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",a.stage,a.output)
    def meta(key):
        return subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(a.output),key],text=True).strip()
    assert (meta("Package"),meta("Version"),meta("Architecture"))==(
        "com.duoface.independent",VER,"iphoneos-arm64")
    report={"status":"NATIVE12_SPEED1_TEST_DEB_BUILT_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
        "base_sha256":HASH,"deb_sha256":sha(a.output),
        "version":VER,"modules":mods,"three_gmap_publishers":list(SRC),
        "old_native12_files_unchanged":True,"no_other_renderer":True,
        "sileo_published":False,"device_tested":False}
    a.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    a.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed1_candidate.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed2hud_candidate.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exact Native12 + one CarPlay HUD dylib; no app adapters, TWHub file overlap or Waze hooks."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="85d754ea887eb8dd0659f65a5b2e3425564f617983937579753bad1a69433bf7"
VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed2hud"
DIR=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
NEW=("DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib","DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist")
BAN=("DuoFaceSpeedSources","WazeGMapHUDSource","GofaGMapHUDSource",
    "VietMapGoogleMapsSource","GMapHUDPlacementRelay","GMapHUDOverspeedScreen","GoogleMapsVietMap")
def hashfile(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def cmd(*args):subprocess.run([str(a) for a in args],check=True)
def entries(root):
    return {f.relative_to(root).as_posix():hashfile(f) for f in root.rglob("*")
      if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    for key in ("hud","output","report"):
        ap.add_argument("--"+key,type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    a=ap.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and hashfile(BASE)==PIN,"Native12 immutable SHA256 mismatch"
    assert a.hud.is_file() and a.hud.stat().st_size>3000
    assert not a.output.exists() and not a.report.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-speed2hud-") as tmp:
        dest=pathlib.Path(tmp)/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,dest)
        before=entries(dest)
        assert len(before)>30
        assert not any(any(name in path for name in BAN) for path in before)
        assert all(str(DIR/(name+".dylib")) in before for name in
          ("DuoFaceDashboard","DuoFaceFullscreenBridge","DuoFaceVisualHook","DuoFaceLogoGripOnly"))
        assert "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI" in before
        destlib=dest/DIR
        dll=destlib/NEW[0]
        plist=destlib/NEW[1]
        assert not dll.exists() and not plist.exists()
        shutil.copy2(a.hud,dll)
        dll.chmod(0o755)
        shutil.copy2("DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist",plist)
        assert b"com.apple.CarPlayApp" in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b"com.apple.CarPlayTemplateUIHost" in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b"com.apple.springboard" not in plist.read_bytes()
        cmd("ldid","-S",dll)
        cmd("lipo",dll,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        cmd("plutil","-lint",plist)
        ctrl=dest/"DEBIAN/control"
        text=ctrl.read_text()
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native12" in text
        ctrl.write_text(text.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native12","Version: "+VERSION,1))
        after=entries(dest)
        assert set(after)-set(before)=={str(DIR/name) for name in NEW}
        assert all(after.get(path)==digest for path,digest in before.items())
        assert not any(any(name in path for name in BAN) for path in after)
        (dest/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("".join(
            hashlib.md5((dest/path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+path+"\n"
            for path in sorted(after)))
        a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",dest,a.output)
        appended={str(DIR/name):after[str(DIR/name)] for name in NEW}
    get=lambda field:subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(a.output),field],text=True).strip()
    assert (get("Package"),get("Version"),get("Architecture"))==(
        "com.duoface.independent",VERSION,"iphoneos-arm64")
    report={"status":"NATIVE12_SPEED2HUD_ISOLATION_DEB_STATIC_PASS_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
      "input_sha256":PIN,"output_sha256":hashfile(a.output),
      "new_module":appended,"original_file_count":len(before),
      "all_original_payload_bytes_unchanged":True,
      "no_third_party_injection_or_waze_hooks":True,
      "activation":"CarPlayApp/CarPlayTemplateUIHost scene/window observer",
      "fallback_display":"visible — placeholders when no fresh speed",
      "limitations":"No speed source adapters, no source selector UI; isolate HUD before next stage",
      "sileo_published":False,"device_tested":False}
    a.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    a.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed2hud_candidate.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed5scene_candidate.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exact Native12 + one SpringBoard-only DuoFace scene HUD; no app or Waze injection."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="85d754ea887eb8dd0659f65a5b2e3425564f617983937579753bad1a69433bf7"
VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed5scene"
DIR=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
NEW=("DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib","DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist")
BAN=("DuoFaceSpeedSources","WazeGMapHUDSource","GofaGMapHUDSource",
    "VietMapGoogleMapsSource","GMapHUDPlacementRelay","GMapHUDOverspeedScreen","GoogleMapsVietMap")
def hashfile(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def cmd(*args):subprocess.run([str(a) for a in args],check=True)
def entries(root):
    return {f.relative_to(root).as_posix():hashfile(f) for f in root.rglob("*")
      if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    for key in ("hud","output","report"):
        ap.add_argument("--"+key,type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    a=ap.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and hashfile(BASE)==PIN,"Native12 immutable SHA256 mismatch"
    assert a.hud.is_file() and a.hud.stat().st_size>3000
    assert not a.output.exists() and not a.report.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-speed5scene-") as tmp:
        dest=pathlib.Path(tmp)/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,dest)
        before=entries(dest)
        assert len(before)>30
        assert not any(any(name in path for name in BAN) for path in before)
        assert all(str(DIR/(name+".dylib")) in before for name in
          ("DuoFaceDashboard","DuoFaceFullscreenBridge","DuoFaceVisualHook","DuoFaceLogoGripOnly"))
        assert "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI" in before
        destlib=dest/DIR
        dll=destlib/NEW[0]
        plist=destlib/NEW[1]
        assert not dll.exists() and not plist.exists()
        shutil.copy2(a.hud,dll)
        dll.chmod(0o755)
        shutil.copy2("DuoFaceSpeedHUDScene.plist",plist)
        assert b'SpringBoard' in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b'com.apple.CarPlayApp' not in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b'com.apple.CarPlayTemplateUIHost' not in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b'com.waze.iphone' not in plist.read_bytes()
        assert plist.read_text().count("SpringBoard")==1
        cmd("ldid","-S",dll)
        cmd("lipo",dll,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        cmd("plutil","-lint",plist)
        ctrl=dest/"DEBIAN/control"
        text=ctrl.read_text()
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native12" in text
        ctrl.write_text(text.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native12","Version: "+VERSION,1))
        after=entries(dest)
        assert set(after)-set(before)=={str(DIR/name) for name in NEW}
        assert all(after.get(path)==digest for path,digest in before.items())
        assert not any(any(name in path for name in BAN) for path in after)
        (dest/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("".join(
            hashlib.md5((dest/path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+path+"\n"
            for path in sorted(after)))
        a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",dest,a.output)
        appended={str(DIR/name):after[str(DIR/name)] for name in NEW}
    get=lambda field:subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(a.output),field],text=True).strip()
    assert (get("Package"),get("Version"),get("Architecture"))==(
        "com.duoface.independent",VERSION,"iphoneos-arm64")
    report={"status":"NATIVE12_SPEED5SCENE_ISOLATION_DEB_STATIC_PASS_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
      "input_sha256":PIN,"output_sha256":hashfile(a.output),
      "new_module":appended,"original_file_count":len(before),
      "all_original_payload_bytes_unchanged":True,
      "no_third_party_injection_or_waze_hooks":True,
      "springboard_only":True,
      "native12_dashboard_owned_scene_runtime_unverified":True,
      "activation":"SpringBoard only; CBBridgeManager.sharedInstanceIfExists.window + CBWindow show/hide/resize/destroy + reconciler; guarded CarPlay UIWindowScene overlay",
      "fallback_display":"GOFA-style transparent separate UIWindow at level 10000000, — placeholders in live native split only; require device screen test",
      "limitations":"No speed source adapters, no source selector UI; host ownership AND HUD visibility require real CarPlay device test",
      "sileo_published":False,"device_tested":False}
    a.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    a.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed5scene_candidate.py ##########

"""Exact Native12 + one CarPlay HUD dylib; no app adapters, TWHub file overlap or Waze hooks."""

import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="85d754ea887eb8dd0659f65a5b2e3425564f617983937579753bad1a69433bf7"
VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed6split1"
DIR=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
NEW=("DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib","DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist")
BAN=("DuoFaceSpeedSources","WazeGMapHUDSource","GofaGMapHUDSource",
    "VietMapGoogleMapsSource","GMapHUDPlacementRelay","GMapHUDOverspeedScreen","GoogleMapsVietMap")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Losslessly preserve released Speed6zProbe1; append only read-only SpringBoard owner module."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile

BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12-speed6probe1/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12+speed6zprobe1_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="42f7300463fa7aeecfb28342298e5faf3c77dcf0319e77964a6b35622eba722e"
VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed6zzowner1"
DIR=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
NEW=("DuoFaceSpeedOwner.dylib","DuoFaceSpeedOwner.plist")
BAN=("DuoFaceSpeedSources","WazeGMapHUDSource","GofaGMapHUDSource","VietMapGoogleMapsSource","GMapHUDPlacementRelay","GMapHUDOverspeedScreen","GoogleMapsVietMap")
def digest(path): return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def cmd(*args): subprocess.run([str(a) for a in args],check=True)
def inventory(root):
    return {str(p.relative_to(root)):digest(p) for p in root.rglob("*")
        if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    for field in ("owner","output","report"):
        parser.add_argument("--"+field,required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    a=parser.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and digest(BASE)==PIN, "Released Speed6zProbe1 exact DEB SHA mismatch"
    assert a.owner.is_file() and a.owner.stat().st_size>3000, "Missing compiled arm64 owner module"
    assert not a.output.exists() and not a.report.exists(), "Do not overwrite prior package"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-ownerprobe-") as td:
        root=pathlib.Path(td)/"payload"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,root)
        before=inventory(root)
        assert len(before)==41,len(before)
        assert not any(any(item in key for item in BAN) for key in before)
        lib=root/DIR
        for p in ("DuoFaceDashboard","DuoFaceFullscreenBridge","DuoFaceVisualHook","DuoFaceLogoGripOnly","DuoFaceSpeedHUD"):
            assert str(DIR/(p+".dylib")) in before,p
        assert str(DIR/"DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist") in before
        assert "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI" in before
        owner=lib/NEW[0]
        plist=lib/NEW[1]
        assert owner.stem==plist.stem
        assert not owner.exists() and not plist.exists()
        shutil.copy2(a.owner,owner)
        owner.chmod(0o755)
        shutil.copy2("DuoFaceSpeedOwner.plist",plist)
        assert b"SpringBoard" in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b"Executables" in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b"CarPlayApp" not in plist.read_bytes()
        cmd("ldid","-S",owner)
        cmd("lipo",owner,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        cmd("plutil","-lint",plist)
        ctrl=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        original=ctrl.read_text()
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native12+speed6zprobe1" in original
        ctrl.write_text(original.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native12+speed6zprobe1","Version: "+VERSION,1))
        after=inventory(root)
        assert set(after)-set(before)=={str(DIR/n) for n in NEW}
        assert all(after[p]==s for p,s in before.items()),"Original CarPlay HUD or Native12 changed"
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("".join(
            hashlib.md5((root/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+p+"\n"
            for p in sorted(after)))
        a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",root,a.output)
        appended={str(DIR/n):after[str(DIR/n)] for n in NEW}
    get=lambda f:subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(a.output),f],text=True).strip()
    assert (get("Package"),get("Version"),get("Architecture"))==(
        "com.duoface.independent",VERSION,"iphoneos-arm64")
    report={
        "status":"SPEED6ZZOWNER1_SPRINGBOARD_OWNER_QA_STATIC_PASS_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
        "input_released_speed6_sha256":PIN,
        "input_native12_lineage_sha256":"85d754ea887eb8dd0659f65a5b2e3425564f617983937579753bad1a69433bf7",
        "output_sha256":digest(a.output),
        "preserved_file_count":len(before),
        "all_previous_speed6_payload_files_byte_identical":True,
        "new_module":appended,
        "new_module_count":len(NEW),
        "springboard_readonly":True,
        "no_springboard_uiwindow_creation_or_method_hooks":True,
        "carplay_hud_injection_and_gates_unchanged":True,
        "no_third_party_injection_or_waze_hooks":True,
        "springboard_probe_log":"/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceSpeedOwner-SpringBoard.jsonl",
        "carplay_existing_log":"/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceSpeed6-CarPlayApp.jsonl",
        "sileo_published":False,
        "device_tested":False,
    }
    a.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    a.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=="__main__":main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Speed7: replace only the old wrong-process CarPlay HUD with in-place SpringBoard split HUD."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12-speed6zzowner1/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12+speed6zzowner1_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="d63cf64a5139c7c235b9836b963f2a6a9f25ac414c4eda79bcea07ddda32475a"
VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed7split"
DIR=pathlib.Path("var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries")
REPLACE=("DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib","DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist")
BAN=("DuoFaceSpeedSources","WazeGMapHUDSource","GofaGMapHUDSource",
     "GMapHUDPlacementRelay","GMapHUDOverspeedScreen","GoogleMapsVietMap")
def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def cmd(*args):subprocess.run([str(v) for v in args],check=True)
def payload(root):
    return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(p) for p in root.rglob("*")
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    for x in ("hud","output","report"):p.add_argument("--"+x,required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    a=p.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE)==PIN,"Immutable installed baseline wrong"
    assert a.hud.is_file() and a.hud.stat().st_size>3000
    assert not a.output.exists() and not a.report.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native-speed7split-") as td:
        root=pathlib.Path(td)/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,root)
        before=payload(root)
        assert len(before)==43,len(before)
        assert not any(any(x in z for x in BAN) for z in before)
        originals={z:d for z,d in before.items() if z not in {str(DIR/n) for n in REPLACE}}
        assert len(originals)==41
        assert str(DIR/"DuoFaceSpeedOwner.dylib") in originals
        assert str(DIR/"DuoFaceSpeedOwner.plist") in originals
        for z in ("DuoFaceDashboard","DuoFaceFullscreenBridge",
                  "DuoFaceVisualHook","DuoFaceLogoGripOnly"):
            assert str(DIR/(z+".dylib")) in originals
        dest=root/DIR
        dll=dest/"DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib"
        plist=dest/"DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist"
        assert dll.is_file() and plist.is_file() and dll.stem==plist.stem
        # Root owner probe remains unchanged; old CarPlayApp renderer deliberately replaced.
        shutil.copy2(a.hud,dll);dll.chmod(0o755)
        shutil.copy2("DuoFaceSpeedHUDNative7.plist",plist)
        assert b"SpringBoard" in plist.read_bytes()
        assert b"CarPlayApp" not in plist.read_bytes()
        cmd("ldid","-S",dll)
        cmd("lipo",dll,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        cmd("plutil","-lint",plist)
        ctrl=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        text=ctrl.read_text()
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native12+speed6zzowner1" in text
        ctrl.write_text(text.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native12+speed6zzowner1","Version: "+VERSION,1))
        after=payload(root)
        assert set(after)==set(before)
        assert all(after[z]==d for z,d in originals.items()),"Unrelated payload changed"
        assert before[str(DIR/"DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib")]!=after[str(DIR/"DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib")]
        assert before[str(DIR/"DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist")]!=after[str(DIR/"DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist")]
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("".join(
          hashlib.md5((root/z).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+z+"\n"
          for z in sorted(after)))
        a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",root,a.output)
        replaced={str(DIR/n):{"before":before[str(DIR/n)],"after":after[str(DIR/n)]} for n in REPLACE}
    get=lambda field:subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(a.output),field],text=True).strip()
    assert (get("Package"),get("Version"),get("Architecture"))==(
        "com.duoface.independent",VERSION,"iphoneos-arm64")
    report={
      "status":"SPEED7_NATIVE12_SPRINGBOARD_SPLIT_RENDERER_QA_STATIC_PASS_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
      "baseline_speed6zzowner1_sha256":PIN,
      "output_sha256":sha(a.output),
      "preserved_files_byte_identical":len(originals),
      "replaced_files_exact_count":len(REPLACE),
      "replaced_only_HUD_dylib_and_plist":True,
      "original_native12_and_springboard_owner_probe_unchanged":True,
      "carplayapp_HUD_injection_removed":True,
      "speed7_filter_springboard_only":True,
      "creates_new_UIWindow":False,
      "requires_native_bridged_split_app_two_attached_panels":True,
      "root_class_verified_from_real_device":"CBTouchPassThroughRootSceneWindow",
      "source_data_independent_placeholder":True,
      "new_hud_files":replaced,
      "runtime_log":"/var/mobile/Library/Logs/DuoFaceSpeedSplit7-SpringBoard.jsonl",
      "sileo_published":False,
      "device_tested":False,
      "risk":"SplitApp.appViews and panel attachment must be checked on real device. Do not claim Home=0/1 split/exit=0 before real test."
    }
    a.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    a.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
