########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speedbubble_candidate.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build a single-bubble SPEEDHUD TEST candidate from EXACT user-tested Native12.

Native12 (sha256 pinned) stays byte-exact in every pre-existing payload file.
This does NOT integrate native13-21. Does not upload to Sileo on its own.
The GMapHUD v0.2.3 source-only publishers remain individually version guarded.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import plistlib
import subprocess
import tempfile

from stage_gmaphud_modules_for_duoface import (
    stage as stage_publishers, NATIVE12_SHA256, GMAP_SHA256, DYLIB_DIR,
)

BASE = Path("dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
VERSION = "0.1.0~native12+speedhud1"
NEW = ("DuoFaceSpeedHUD", "DuoFaceSpeedSources")
FILTERS = {
    "DuoFaceSpeedHUD": {"com.apple.springboard"},
    "DuoFaceSpeedSources": {
        "com.google.Maps", "com.waze.iphone",
        "vn.vietmap.live", "com.lumi.GOFA",
    },
}
PROTECTED = (
    "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib",
    "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceFullscreenBridge.dylib",
    "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib",
    "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.dylib",
    "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",
)
FORBIDDEN = ("GMapHUDOverspeedScreen", "GMapHUDPlacementRelay",
             "GoogleMapsVietMap", "DuoFaceInCarAddApps", "DuoFaceSmartConnection")

def sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def run(*args: str) -> None:
    subprocess.run(args, check=True)

def dpkg_field(deb: Path, key: str) -> str:
    return subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(deb), key], text=True).strip()

def snapshot(root: Path) -> dict:
    files = {}
    for p in root.rglob("*"):
        r = p.relative_to(root)
        if r.parts[0] == "DEBIAN":
            continue
        if p.is_symlink():
            files[r.as_posix()] = {"symlink": p.readlink().as_posix()}
        elif p.is_file():
            files[r.as_posix()] = {"sha256": sha(p), "mode": p.stat().st_mode & 0o7777}
    return files

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument("--gmaphud", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--hud-dylib", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--source-dylib", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report", type=Path, required=True)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE)==NATIVE12_SHA256, "Wrong Native12! No native13+"
    assert args.gmaphud.is_file() and sha(args.gmaphud)==GMAP_SHA256, "Wrong GMapHUD"
    assert not args.out.exists() and not args.report.exists(), "Never overwrite an existing candidate"
    assert dpkg_field(BASE,"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert dpkg_field(BASE,"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
    assert dpkg_field(BASE,"Version")=="0.1.0~native12"
    run("dpkg","--compare-versions",VERSION,"gt","0.1.0~native12")
    run("dpkg","--compare-versions",VERSION,"lt","0.1.0~native13")
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native12-speed1-") as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        stage = root/"stage"
        import_report = root/"import.json"
        import_data = stage_publishers(args.gmaphud,stage,import_report,BASE)
        assert import_data["single_duoface_hud_only"]
        assert len(import_data["copied"])==8
        before_root = root/"native12-ref"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",str(BASE),str(before_root))
        original = snapshot(before_root)
        assert len(original)>30
        for protected in PROTECTED:
            assert protected in original, f"Native12 protected host missing: {protected}"
        newly_added = set(import_data["copied"])
        libdir = stage/DYLIB_DIR
        compiled = {}
        for name,compiled_path in zip(NEW,(args.hud_dylib,args.source_dylib)):
            assert compiled_path.is_file() and compiled_path.stat().st_size>2000
            relative = (DYLIB_DIR/(name+".dylib")).as_posix()
            filter_relative = (DYLIB_DIR/(name+".plist")).as_posix()
            dest = stage/relative
            f = stage/filter_relative
            assert not dest.exists() and not f.exists()
            dest.write_bytes(compiled_path.read_bytes())
            dest.chmod(0o755)
            f.write_bytes(Path(name+".plist").read_bytes())
            assert set(plistlib.loads(f.read_bytes())["Filter"]["Bundles"]) == FILTERS[name]
            run("ldid","-S",str(dest))
            run("lipo",str(dest),"-verify_arch","arm64")
            run("plutil","-lint",str(f))
            compiled[name] = {"dylib":relative,"dylib_sha256":sha(dest),
                              "plist":filter_relative,"plist_sha256":sha(f)}
            newly_added.update((relative,filter_relative))
        after = snapshot(stage)
        for bad in FORBIDDEN:
            assert not any(bad in p for p in after),f"Forbidden other-version/duplicate UI: {bad}"
        assert all(k in after and v==after[k] for k,v in original.items()), "Native12 file modified!"
        assert set(after)-set(original)==newly_added, (
            "Wrong set of added files",sorted(set(after)-set(original)),sorted(newly_added))
        assert not (libdir/"A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease.dylib").exists()
        control = stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        old_control=control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native12" in old_control
        control.write_text(old_control.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native12",
                              "Version: "+VERSION,1),encoding="utf-8")
        # No hidden full-app reprovisioning: all byte-identical baseline files remain.
        md5=[]
        for rel in sorted(after):
            f=stage/rel
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink():
                md5.append(hashlib.md5(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+rel)
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(stage),str(args.out))
    assert dpkg_field(args.out,"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert dpkg_field(args.out,"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
    assert dpkg_field(args.out,"Version")==VERSION
    report={
        "status":"BUILT_NATIVE12_SPEEDHUD1_TEST_CANDIDATE_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
        "base":"dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb",
        "base_sha256":NATIVE12_SHA256,
        "gmaphud_023_sha256":GMAP_SHA256,
        "version":VERSION,
        "candidate_sha256":sha(args.out),
        "candidate_size":args.out.stat().st_size,
        "all_original_payload_byte_identical":True,
        "original_payload_count":len(original),
        "added_exact_count":len(newly_added),
        "added_source_files":import_data["copied"],
        "added_duoface_tweaks":compiled,
        "no_second_hud_renderer":True,
        "google_maps_speed_limit_implemented":False,
        "user_installs_extra_hud_tweaks":False,
        "sileo_published":False,
        "device_tested":False,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=="__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speedbubble_candidate.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_without_lease_bridge.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Create reversible native12 trial removing the ENTIRE obsolete license-only hook.

This removes the separate A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease.dylib and its injection
.plist from the signed native11 package. It never truncates or renames any
Objective-C class/selector data in the main Dashboard or application Mach-O.

Rationale: all 10 explicit callback target methods in the hook's published
source already have ABI-compatible replacement bodies in native4. Still,
cross-process dynamic references cannot be ruled out by this static audit.
This is a CANDIDATE; not proven to work until actual device tests.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import tempfile

from build_native5 import cmd, sha
from build_native8 import files_and_digests

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
BASE=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native11/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native11_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
BASE_SHA="f51180308e7bfd3a86af011c7370290eee4be59d6c7aced5c5ff790e685dc2c5"
NATIVE10_SHA="3d39b181d0089ae9a7797eb1ca05790155d1b242e50ca2cff362e0a818ff8d29"
VERSION="0.1.0~native12"
PREFIX="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"
REMOVALS={
    PREFIX+"A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease.dylib",
    PREFIX+"A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease.plist",
}
HOOK=ROOT/"migration/full_payload/native_test/DuoFaceNoLeaseBridge.mm"
NEUTRALIZED=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native4/BAO_CAO.json"

EXPECTED_MANAGER={
    "isLicenseCurrentlyValid","remainingLicenseSeconds",
    "actualLicenseExpirationTimestamp","checkAndSyncLicenseIfNeeded",
    "checkAndSyncLicenseIfNeededWithSource:","resolvedLicenseServerEndpoint",
}
EXPECTED_BRIDGE={
    "startLicenseWatchdogTimer","stopLicenseWatchdogTimer",
    "licenseWatchdogTimerFired:","presentLicenseWarningModalWithType:",
}

def source_check() -> dict:
    source=HOOK.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "DFITargetProcess" in source and "DFIHookMethod" in source
    a=set(re.findall(r'DFIInstallMethodForValue\(manager,@"([^"]+)"',source))
    b=set(re.findall(r'DFIHookMethod\(manager,@"([^"]+)"',source))
    c=set(re.findall(r'DFIHookMethod\(bridge,@"([^"]+)"',source))
    assert a|b==EXPECTED_MANAGER,(a|b,EXPECTED_MANAGER)
    assert c==EXPECTED_BRIDGE,(c,EXPECTED_BRIDGE)
    assert len(a)+len(b)+len(c)==10
    patched=json.loads(NEUTRALIZED.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["method_bodies"]
    summary={}
    for binary,obj in patched.items():
        methods=obj["patched_methods"]
        expected={(m["class"],m["selector"]) for m in methods}
        for selector in EXPECTED_MANAGER:
            assert ("CBLicenseSyncManager",selector) in expected,(binary,selector)
        if binary=="DuoFaceDashboard":
            for selector in EXPECTED_BRIDGE:
                assert ("CBBridgeManager",selector) in expected,(binary,selector)
        summary[binary]={
            "license_manager_hook_selectors_previously_neutralized":len(EXPECTED_MANAGER),
            "bridge_hook_selectors_previously_neutralized":len(EXPECTED_BRIDGE)
            if binary=="DuoFaceDashboard" else 0,
        }
    return summary

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=Path)
    ap.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=Path)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE.read_bytes())==BASE_SHA
    assert not args.out.exists(),"Refuse overwrite of package or previous release"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Version"],text=True).strip()=="0.1.0~native11"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Architecture"],text=True).strip()=="iphoneos-arm64"
    source_trace=source_check()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native12-strip-module-") as td:
        stage=Path(td)/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,stage)
        before=files_and_digests(stage)
        assert set(REMOVALS)<=set(before),"Baseline module or filter absent"
        orig_control=(stage/"DEBIAN/control").read_text()
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native11" in orig_control
        sizes={}
        for relative in REMOVALS:
            file=stage/relative
            assert file.is_file() and not file.is_symlink()
            sizes[relative]=file.stat().st_size
            file.unlink()
        assert sizes[PREFIX+"A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease.dylib"]>1000
        # Check all other files, including DEBIAN scripts, for literal
        # dynamic-load references. A runtime ObjC callback is a separate risk.
        any_refs={}
        needle=b"A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease"
        for f in stage.rglob("*"):
            # md5sums is *expected* to mention removed files until the next
            # step regenerates it. All executable/config/maintainer files are
            # still searched; only the stale inventory is excluded.
            if f.relative_to(stage).as_posix()=="DEBIAN/md5sums":
                continue
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and needle in f.read_bytes():
                any_refs[f.relative_to(stage).as_posix()]=True
        assert not any_refs,any_refs
        control=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        control.write_text(orig_control.replace(
            "Version: 0.1.0~native11","Version: "+VERSION),encoding="utf-8")
        checksums=[]
        for f in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(stage).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                checksums.append(hashlib.md5(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+
                                 "  "+f.relative_to(stage).as_posix())
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(checksums)+"\n")
        after=files_and_digests(stage)
        assert set(before)-set(after)==REMOVALS
        assert not (set(after)-set(before))
        changed=sorted(k for k in before if k in after and before[k]!=after[k])
        assert changed==["DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"],changed
        assert len(before)-len(after)==2
        assert len(before)>30
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",stage,args.out)
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Architecture"],text=True).strip()=="iphoneos-arm64"
    report={
        "status":"NATIVE12_WHOLE_OBSOLETE_LEASE_HOOK_LIBRARY_AND_FILTER_REMOVED_FROM_REAL_DEB",
        "source_native11_sha256":BASE_SHA,
        "source_native10_verified_by_user_sha256":NATIVE10_SHA,
        "package":"com.duoface.independent","version":VERSION,
        "architecture":"iphoneos-arm64",
        "files_physically_removed":sorted(REMOVALS),
        "bytes_removed_uncompressed":sum(sizes.values()),
        "size_by_removed_file":sizes,
        "selector_hooks_previously_neutralized_in_native4":source_trace,
        "matching_hook_selector_count":10,
        "all_other_payload_files_byte_identical":True,
        "all_8_carplay_method_bodies_byte_identical":True,
        "all_app_and_dashboard_code_and_objc_metadata_byte_identical":True,
        "full_screen_logo_divider_ui_unchanged":True,
        "main_macho_license_class_metadata_still_present":True,
        "all_license_code_and_metadata_fully_removed":False,
        "dynamic_objc_or_process_dependency_proven_absent":False,
        "only_existing_files_changed":changed,
        "baseline_module_load_name_references_in_other_files":any_refs,
        "new_deb_on_sileo":False,"device_tested":False,
        "native10_and_native11_rollback_preserved":True,
        "deb_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "deb_size":args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=="__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_without_lease_bridge.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native13_features_candidate.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build an UNPUBLISHED native13 QA candidate from immutable native12 and two
separately compiled/signature-checked DuoFace-only connection and picker hooks.

Never claims license metadata was removed; physical excision has its own QA gate.
No Sileo publishing, no change to native12 and no implicit destructive cleanup.
"""
import argparse, hashlib, json, os, pathlib, shutil, subprocess, tempfile

BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
SHA="85d754ea887eb8dd0659f65a5b2e3425564f617983937579753bad1a69433bf7"
VERSION="0.1.0~native13"
ROOT="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries"
ADDED=("DuoFaceSmartConnection","DuoFaceInCarAddApps")
PROTECTED=(
  "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib",
  "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceFullscreenBridge.dylib",
  "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib",
  "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.dylib",
  "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",
)
def run(*args):
    subprocess.run(args,check=True,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
def digest(data):return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
def collect(stage):
    return {f.relative_to(stage).as_posix():digest(f.read_bytes()) for f in stage.rglob("*")
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(stage).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--smart-dylib",required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    ap.add_argument("--app-dylib",required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    ap.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    ap.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert digest(BASE.read_bytes())==SHA,"immutable native12 DEB hash mismatch"
    assert not args.out.exists(),"native13 candidate must not overwrite"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Version"],text=True).strip()=="0.1.0~native12"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native13-qa-only-") as tmp:
        root=pathlib.Path(tmp)/"root"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",str(BASE),str(root))
        orig=collect(root)
        for rel in PROTECTED:
            assert rel in orig,rel
        libdir=root/ROOT
        added={}
        for name,src in zip(ADDED,(args.smart_dylib,args.app_dylib)):
            assert src.is_file() and len(src.read_bytes())>2000,src
            dylib=libdir/(name+".dylib")
            filt=libdir/(name+".plist")
            assert not dylib.exists() and not filt.exists(),(dylib,filt)
            shutil.copy2(src,dylib)
            shutil.copy2(pathlib.Path(name+".plist"),filt)
            # Standalone Theos outputs need valid iOS code signature to inject.
            run("ldid","-S",str(dylib))
            run("lipo",str(dylib),"-verify_arch","arm64")
            run("plutil","-lint",str(filt))
            added[name]={"dylib":f"{ROOT}/{name}.dylib","plist":f"{ROOT}/{name}.plist",
                         "sha256":digest(dylib.read_bytes()),"filter_sha256":digest(filt.read_bytes())}
        control=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        s=control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native12" in s
        control.write_text(s.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native12",f"Version: {VERSION}",1),encoding="utf-8")
        current=collect(root)
        unexpected=set(current)-set(orig)-{f"{ROOT}/{n}.{ext}" for n in ADDED for ext in ("dylib","plist")}
        assert not unexpected,unexpected
        assert all(current[k]==v for k,v in orig.items()),"native12 legacy file modified"
        assert all(current.get(rel)==orig[rel] for rel in PROTECTED)
        assert f"{ROOT}/A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease.dylib" not in current
        # Rebuild package member checksum list with the exact updated payload.
        md5=[]
        for path in sorted(current):
            payload=(root/path).read_bytes()
            md5.append(f"{hashlib.md5(payload).hexdigest()}  {path}")
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5)+"\n")
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(root),str(args.out))
        report={
         "status":"NATIVE13_TWO_FEATURES_SIGNED_COMBINED_QA_CANDIDATE_NOT_PUBLISHED",
         "package":"com.duoface.independent","version":VERSION,
         "input_native12_sha256":SHA,"candidate_sha256":digest(args.out.read_bytes()),
         "added_modules":added,"existing_payload_entries_unchanged":len(orig),
         "all_original_payload_byte_identical":True,
         "all_five_carplay_and_visual_hosts_byte_identical":True,
         "old_license_hook_still_absent":True,
         "remaining_license_class_metadata_physically_removed":False,
         "full_license_excision_verified":False,
         "sileo_published":False,"device_tested":False,
         "must_not_publish_as_complete_license_excision":True
        }
        args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
        print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native14_license_class_trial.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Physical trial excision of 14 orphan license/subscription ObjC class records.
Experimental DEVICE-TEST CANDIDATE, not proof of runtime liveness or FULL
license-removal. Input is native13 with two features; native12 stays immutable.
"""
import argparse,hashlib,json,os,pathlib,subprocess,tempfile
from audit_native12_license_class_xrefs import MachO,select_arm64,BINARIES
ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
BASE=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native13/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native13_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
BASE_SHA="cff76573be10a73bfd423e988b803812e3375af4db6567a9229c1b04156f2cf3"
AUDIT=ROOT/"audit/payload-migration/NATIVE12_ACTUAL_LICENSE_CLASS_XREFS.json"
SOURCE=ROOT/"audit/payload-migration/NATIVE12_LICENSE_XREF_CALLSITE_OWNERS.json"
VERSION="0.1.0~native14"
def sha(b):return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def run(*a):
 p=subprocess.run(a,text=True,capture_output=True)
 if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError(f"{a}: {p.stdout[-1000:]} {p.stderr[-2000:]}")
 return p.stdout
def patch_host(original,classes):
 m=MachO(select_arm64(original));out=bytearray(original)
 original_class_names=set(m.classes())
 if len(classes) not in (2,12):raise ValueError("Unexpected proposed count")
 done=[];used=set()
 for item in classes:
  name=item["class"]; pointer=int(item["class_ptr"],16)
  assert name in original_class_names,(name,"original class missing")
  assert not item["safe_to_delete"],"Audit marks this unproven dynamic liveness"
  assert len(item["pointer_references"])==3
  actual_roles={p["role"] for p in item["pointer_references"]}
  assert actual_roles=={"class_registration","direct_reference"}
  for p in item["pointer_references"]:
   off=p["fileoff"]
   assert 0<off<len(out)-8 and off not in used
   assert int.from_bytes(out[off:off+8],"little")==pointer,(name,off,"pointer mismatch")
   out[off:off+8]=b"\x00"*8
   used.add(off)
  seen=0
  for pos in item["literal_file_offsets"]:
   expected=name.encode("ascii")
   assert out[pos:pos+len(expected)+1]==expected+b"\x00",(name,pos)
   out[pos:pos+len(expected)]=b"\x00"*len(expected)
   seen+=1
  # The former xref report lists class-name metadata literals but does not
  # claim the list is complete. Only erase additional NUL-terminated literal
  # occurrences if independently verified inside a non-instruction string
  # section. Never alter __text, method instructions, or arbitrary binary data.
  extra=[]
  embedded=[]
  needle=name.encode("ascii")
  for sec in m.sections:
   if sec["name"] not in ("__cstring","__objc_classname","__objc_methname"):
    continue
   off=sec["fileoff"]; end=off+sec["size"]
   cursor=off
   while True:
    pos=out.find(needle,cursor,end)
    if pos<0:break
    cursor=pos+len(needle)
    # Prefixes in protocol names or ObjC type encodings are NOT the class
    # name literal. Preserve these unrelated identifiers exactly.
    if pos+len(needle)>=end or out[pos+len(needle)]!=0:
     embedded.append({"file_offset":pos,"section":sec["name"],"why":"prefix of longer token"})
     continue
    if pos>off and out[pos-1] not in (0,32,34,64,91):
     embedded.append({"file_offset":pos,"section":sec["name"],"why":"embedded in other token"})
     continue
    out[pos:pos+len(needle)]=b"\x00"*len(needle)
    extra.append({"file_offset":pos,"section":sec["name"]})
  done.append({"class":name,"registry_and_ref_slots_zeroed":3,
               "name_literals_zeroed":seen+len(extra),"extra_validated_literals":extra,"preserved_embedded_protocol_or_type_names":embedded,
               "method_records_persist_in_unregistered_metadata":item["method_count"]})
 result=bytes(out)
 after=MachO(select_arm64(result))
 actual=set(after.classes())
 assert not set(item["class"] for item in classes)&actual
 assert actual==original_class_names-set(item["class"] for item in classes)
 # No registered class or standalone class-name literal may remain.
 # Do NOT confuse protocol names (e.g. CBSubscriptionCheckoutCellDelegate)
 # or method type encodings with the excised class itself.
 for item in classes:
  needle=item["class"].encode("ascii")
  at=0
  while True:
   at=result.find(needle,at)
   if at<0:break
   right=result[at+len(needle):at+len(needle)+1]
   left=result[at-1:at] if at else b""
   if right in (b"\x00",b"") and left in (b"",b"\x00",b" ",b'"',b"@",b"<",b"["):
    raise RuntimeError("EXACT_CLASSNAME_LITERAL_REMAINS "+item["class"]+" "+hex(at))
   at+=len(needle)
 for s in m.sections:
  if s["name"]=="__text":
   assert original[s["fileoff"]:s["fileoff"]+s["size"]]==result[s["fileoff"]:s["fileoff"]+s["size"]]
 return result,done,len(original_class_names),len(actual)
def md5list(stage):
 out=[]
 for f in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
  if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(stage).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
   out.append(f"{hashlib.md5(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}  {f.relative_to(stage).as_posix()}")
 return "\n".join(out)+"\n"
def main():
 ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
 ap.add_argument("--out",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
 ap.add_argument("--report",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
 a=ap.parse_args()
 assert sha(BASE.read_bytes())==BASE_SHA,(sha(BASE.read_bytes()),"pin native13")
 assert not a.out.exists()
 report=json.loads(AUDIT.read_text())
 assert report["candidates_checked"]==14 and not report["physical_excision_completed"]
 owners=json.loads(SOURCE.read_text())
 assert owners["status"]=="NATIVE12_LICENSE_CLASSREF_CALLSITE_OWNERS_READONLY"
 # 3 direct ARM64 references are in ONE previously neutralized ObjC method.
 links=owners["binaries"]["DuoFaceUI"]["xref_owners"]
 assert len(links)==3
 assert {p["method_owner"]["method"] for p in links}=={"-checkAndPresentLicenseModalIfNeeded"}
 assert {p["method_owner"]["class"] for p in links}=={"XXRootViewController"}
 assert owners["binaries"]["DuoFaceDashboard"]["xref_owners"]==[]
 with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native14-license-class-excision-") as tmp:
  root=pathlib.Path(tmp)/"unpack"
  run("dpkg-deb","-R",str(BASE),str(root))
  beforeControl=(root/"DEBIAN/control").read_text()
  assert "Version: 0.1.0~native13" in beforeControl
  details={};changed=set()
  for bin,path in BINARIES.items():
   target=root/path
   raw=target.read_bytes()
   old_sig=run("ldid","-e",str(target)).strip()
   patched,classes,oldcount,newcount=patch_host(raw,report["binaries"][bin]["candidate_classes"])
   target.write_bytes(patched)
   # Preserve every original entitlement if present; never downgrade privileges.
   if old_sig.startswith("<?xml") or "<plist" in old_sig:
    ent=pathlib.Path(tmp)/(bin+".entitlements")
    ent.write_text(old_sig)
    run("ldid","-S"+str(ent),str(target))
   elif not old_sig:
    run("ldid","-S",str(target))
   else:
    raise RuntimeError("unrecognized entitlement output for "+bin)
   # Resigning may resize LC_CODE_SIGNATURE but must not change class/object code.
   m=MachO(select_arm64(target.read_bytes()))
   assert len(m.classes())==newcount
   details[bin]={"before_registered_classes":oldcount,"after_registered_classes":newcount,
                 "removed_classes":classes,"host_sha256":sha(target.read_bytes()),
                 "signed":True,"all_original_text_instructions_unchanged_pre_signature":True}
   changed.add(path)
  (root/"DEBIAN/control").write_text(beforeControl.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native13",
                                                            "Version: "+VERSION,1))
  (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text(md5list(root))
  a.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(root),str(a.out))
  assert run("dpkg-deb","-f",str(a.out),"Version").strip()==VERSION
  result={
    "status":"NATIVE14_EXPERIMENTAL_14_LICENSE_CLASSES_UNREGISTERED_AND_NAMES_ERASED",
    "package":"com.duoface.independent","version":VERSION,
    "base_native13_sha256":BASE_SHA,"deb_sha256":sha(a.out.read_bytes()),
    "isolated_license_classes_removed_from_objc_registration":sum(
      len(z["removed_classes"]) for z in details.values()),
    "class_registration_and_ref_pointer_slots_zeroed":42,
    "license_class_name_literal_copies_erased":sum(
      z["name_literals_zeroed"] for b in details.values() for z in b["removed_classes"]),
    "remaining_method_metadata_and_shared_license_literals_may_exist":True,
    "all_license_related_code_physically_removed":False,
    "dynamic_runtime_safety_proven":False,
    "remaining_8_carplay_methods_preserved_code_sections":True,
    "license_xref_calls_are_in_previously_neutralized_entry_method":True,
    "source_code_instruction_changes":False,
    "host_modified_paths":sorted(changed),
    "binaries":details,
    "sileo_deployed":False,"device_tested":False,
    "experimental_only_do_not_claim_full_license_cleanup":True
  }
  a.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  a.report.write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
  print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!="binaries"},indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native14_license_class_trial.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native15_restore_license_classes.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emergency native15 from exact native13, undo only native14 experimental ObjC class erasure.
Preserve signed 2 feature modules and immutable native12 host binaries.
This is NOT a restoration of old neutralized 312 license method bodies.
"""
import argparse, hashlib, json, pathlib, re, subprocess, tempfile
BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native13/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native13_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
ORIGINAL=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native12/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native12_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
SOURCE_SHA="cff76573be10a73bfd423e988b803812e3375af4db6567a9229c1b04156f2cf3"
ORIGINAL_SHA="85d754ea887eb8dd0659f65a5b2e3425564f617983937579753bad1a69433bf7"
VERSION="0.1.0~native15"
DIR="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"
UI="var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI"
DASH=DIR+"DuoFaceDashboard.dylib"
MODS=("DuoFaceSmartConnection","DuoFaceInCarAddApps")
def sha(data):return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
def cmd(*args):return subprocess.check_output(args,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,text=True).strip()
def files(root):return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes()) for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    a.add_argument("--out",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    a.add_argument("--report",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    p=a.parse_args()
    assert sha(BASE.read_bytes())==SOURCE_SHA,"native13 candidate input hash mismatch"
    assert sha(ORIGINAL.read_bytes())==ORIGINAL_SHA,"native12 tested baseline hash mismatch"
    assert cmd("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert cmd("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Version")=="0.1.0~native13"
    assert not p.out.exists(),"candidate must not overwrite"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native15-recovery-") as td:
        stage=pathlib.Path(td)/"native15"
        base12=pathlib.Path(td)/"native12"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-R",str(BASE),str(stage)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(ORIGINAL),str(base12)],check=True)
        initial=files(stage);ref12=files(base12)
        assert set(initial)-set(ref12)=={DIR+x+ext for x in MODS for ext in (".dylib",".plist")}
        assert all(initial[k]==value for k,value in ref12.items()),"native13 host drift from tested native12"
        assert UI in initial and DASH in initial
        for m in MODS:
            assert DIR+m+".dylib" in initial and DIR+m+".plist" in initial
        assert not any("A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease" in k for k in initial)
        # Native14 altered only these hosts. Native15 source is pre-excision native13
        # and must preserve their exact bytes, not patch a corrupted metadata table.
        ui_bytes=(stage/UI).read_bytes()
        dash_bytes=(stage/DASH).read_bytes()
        from audit_native12_license_class_xrefs import MachO, select_arm64
        classes_ui=MachO(select_arm64(ui_bytes)).classes()
        classes_dash=MachO(select_arm64(dash_bytes)).classes()
        restored_ui=(
            "CBLicenseExpiredModalViewController","CBSubscriptionCheckoutCell",
            "CBSubscriptionGiftcodeInputCell","CBSubscriptionGiftcodeRedeemCell",
            "CBSubscriptionHardwareIDCell","CBSubscriptionOrderCoordinator",
            "CBSubscriptionPendingOrderCell","CBSubscriptionPlanCardCell",
            "CBSubscriptionStatusCell","CBSubscriptionViewController",
            "CBSubscriptionVIPCardCell","CBSubscriptionVIPStatusCell",
        )
        restored_dash=("CBLicenseShieldIconView","CBLicenseWarningModalView")
        assert all(c in classes_ui for c in restored_ui), "12 original UI license classes not all registered"
        assert all(c in classes_dash for c in restored_dash), "2 original dashboard license classes missing"
        assert len(classes_ui)==27 and len(classes_dash)==36, "original ObjC class counts have changed"
        ctl=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        raw=ctl.read_text()
        assert re.search(r"(?m)^Version: 0\.1\.0~native13$",raw)
        ctl.write_text(re.sub(r"(?m)^Version: 0\.1\.0~native13$",f"Version: {VERSION}",raw,count=1))
        after=files(stage)
        assert after==initial, "original app/dylib bytes changed during repackaging"
        for k in (UI,DASH):
            assert after[k]==ref12[k]
        checks=[]
        for name in sorted(after):
            checks.append(hashlib.md5((stage/name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+name)
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(checks)+"\n")
        p.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(stage),str(p.out)],check=True)
        assert cmd("dpkg-deb","-f",str(p.out),"Version")==VERSION
        assert cmd("dpkg-deb","-f",str(p.out),"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
        assert cmd("dpkg-deb","-f",str(p.out),"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"
        report={
           "status":"NATIVE15_RESTORED_14_CLASS_REGISTRATIONS_FROM_NATIVE13_FEATURES_PRESERVED",
           "package":"com.duoface.independent","version":VERSION,
           "native15_sha256":sha(p.out.read_bytes()),"source_native13_sha256":SOURCE_SHA,
           "known_device_working_native12_sha256":ORIGINAL_SHA,
           "input_native14_experimental_license_class_removal_reused":False,
           "restored_original_objc_license_classes":14,
           "restored_UI_class_names":list(restored_ui),"restored_Dashboard_class_names":list(restored_dash),
           "registered_UI_classes":len(classes_ui),"registered_Dashboard_classes":len(classes_dash),
           "both_new_features_present":True,
           "two_signed_hook_modules":{m:{"dylib_sha256":after[DIR+m+".dylib"],"plist_sha256":after[DIR+m+".plist"]} for m in MODS},
           "all_legacy_payload_entries_exactly_equal_to_native12":True,
           "all_native13_payload_entries_unchanged":True,
           "older_native12_preexisting_license_method_stubs_restored":False,
           "complete_original_license_system_restored":False,
           "device_tested":False,"sileo_published":False,
           "warning":"Only undo experimental native14 erasure; native12 pre-existing method neutralizations remain, because native12 was last known device-working baseline."
        }
        p.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        p.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
        print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native15_restore_license_classes.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native16_carplay_app_picker_fix.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Create native16 from EXACT published native15, replacing only CarPlay Add Apps hook + plist.
Never touch Dopamine, native12/15 installed bytes, or existing license machinery.
"""
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,plistlib,re,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
BASE=pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native15/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native15_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
BASE_SHA="8d111e638dce357dfb1187c5b10fdf776d7485c95198ad4cf248d6b51d1740e3"
ROOT="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"
MOD=ROOT+"DuoFaceInCarAddApps"
HOSTS=("var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",ROOT+"DuoFaceDashboard.dylib")
SHA_HOSTS=("cb40346d8ca504cb399abe31367847b4f980a01ba93810986ce1acf1fbe9eef9",
           "9e02a4bcee98fb703777122c67286158d9ca9cfa3088887b5ca280c580a695ea")
VERSION="0.1.0~native16"
def sha(raw):return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
def command(*argv):return subprocess.check_output(argv,text=True,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).strip()
def inventory(root):
 return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes()) for p in root.rglob("*")
         if p.is_file() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
 ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
 ap.add_argument("--picker-dylib",required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
 ap.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
 ap.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=pathlib.Path)
 args=ap.parse_args()
 assert sha(BASE.read_bytes())==BASE_SHA,"Native15 published input SHA mismatch"
 assert command("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"
 assert command("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Version")=="0.1.0~native15"
 assert not args.out.exists(),"Never overwrite existing DEB"
 assert len(args.picker_dylib.read_bytes())>2000
 with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native16-picker-fix-") as tmp:
  stage=pathlib.Path(tmp)/"stage"
  subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-R",str(BASE),str(stage)],check=True)
  original=inventory(stage)
  assert all(original.get(name)==value for name,value in zip(HOSTS,SHA_HOSTS))
  assert ROOT+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib" in original
  assert ROOT+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.plist" in original
  assert MOD+".dylib" in original and MOD+".plist" in original
  new_module=stage/(MOD+".dylib")
  new_filter=stage/(MOD+".plist")
  shutil.copy2(args.picker_dylib,new_module)
  shutil.copy2(pathlib.Path("DuoFaceInCarAddApps.plist"),new_filter)
  # Sign only the independent replacement module.
  subprocess.run(["ldid","-S",str(new_module)],check=True)
  subprocess.run(["lipo",str(new_module),"-verify_arch","arm64"],check=True)
  filt=plistlib.loads(new_filter.read_bytes())["Filter"]
  expected={"SpringBoard","CarPlay","CarPlayApp","Dashboard"}
  assert set(filt["Executables"])==expected,filt
  assert set(filt["Bundles"])=={"com.apple.springboard","com.apple.CarPlay",
   "com.apple.CarPlayApp","com.apple.dashboard"},filt
  current=inventory(stage)
  assert set(current)==set(original)
  changed={k for k in current if current[k]!=original[k]}
  assert changed=={MOD+".dylib",MOD+".plist"},changed
  assert all(current.get(name)==value for name,value in zip(HOSTS,SHA_HOSTS))
  assert current[ROOT+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib"]==original[ROOT+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib"]
  assert current[ROOT+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.plist"]==original[ROOT+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.plist"]
  ctl=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
  text=ctl.read_text()
  assert re.search(r"(?m)^Version: 0\.1\.0~native15$",text)
  ctl.write_text(re.sub(r"(?m)^Version: 0\.1\.0~native15$",f"Version: {VERSION}",text,count=1))
  (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(hashlib.md5((stage/k).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+k for k in sorted(current))+"\n")
  args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(stage),str(args.out)],check=True)
  assert command("dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version")==VERSION
  assert command("dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
  report={"status":"NATIVE16_FIXED_APP_PICKER_HOST_INJECTION_QA_CANDIDATE",
    "package":"com.duoface.independent","version":VERSION,
    "published_native15_base_sha256":BASE_SHA,"native16_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
    "changed_payload_paths":sorted(changed),"preserved_legacy_payload_count":len(original)-len(changed),
    "preserved_original_host_sha256":dict(zip(HOSTS,SHA_HOSTS)),
    "preserved_smart_connection_dylib_sha256":current[ROOT+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib"],
    "new_picker_dylib_sha256":current[MOD+".dylib"],
    "new_filter_sha256":current[MOD+".plist"],
    "injection_executables":sorted(expected),"injection_bundles":sorted(filt["Bundles"]),
    "native15_likely_missed_CarPlayApp_Dashboard_process":True,
    "same_original_preferences_policy_preserved":True,
    "dopamine_configuration_touched":False,"jailbreak_tweaks_run":False,
    "native12_and_native15_untouched":True,
    "device_tested":False,"sileo_published":False,
    "limitations":"Actual process, preferences location, and picker persistence must be tested on iPhone."}
  args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
  print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native17_settings_ui_parity.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Repackage native16 as native17 with exactly one DuoFaceUI-only presentation module.

DuoFaceUI and Dashboard are EXACT native12 byte sequences. Do not change
license method bodies/ObjC metadata, old NoLease hook, CarPlay, SmartConnection,
or 10-second disconnect logic. Source DEB is pinned and never overwritten.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import plistlib
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile

BASE = pathlib.Path("dist/payload-migration/native16/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native16_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
BASE_SHA = "5d5cbd368c24d0c074b081d16aaf7d006467c3e9cfef01ee12378ce79d716fae"
VERSION = "0.1.0~native17"
PREFIX = "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"
MOD = PREFIX + "DuoFaceSettingsUIRestore"
UI = "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI"
DASH = PREFIX + "DuoFaceDashboard.dylib"
EXPECTED_HOSTS = {
    UI: "cb40346d8ca504cb399abe31367847b4f980a01ba93810986ce1acf1fbe9eef9",
    DASH: "9e02a4bcee98fb703777122c67286158d9ca9cfa3088887b5ca280c580a695ea",
}
EXPECTED_SMART = "0986cf70b4c613ad011520204337e1e2b403d24111f39f14f70628fb57e61d50"
EXPECTED_CARPLAY = "d0246415f4020f353e5ec7373d8d375cbbda868471fd1a467f8e57654c4d01ad"

def sha(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

def cmd(*args: str) -> str:
    return subprocess.check_output(args, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True).strip()

def inventory(root: pathlib.Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix(): sha(p.read_bytes())
            for p in root.rglob("*")
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0] != "DEBIAN"}

def check_source_only_ui(source: str) -> None:
    required = ("CBGeneralSettingsViewController", "DFIsObsoleteLicenseRow",
                "DFVisibleCount", "DFVisibleCell", "DFVisibleSelect",
                "com.duoface.duofaceui")
    assert all(word in source for word in required), "Expected native2 UI row filtering missing"
    forbidden = ("CBLicenseSyncManager", "checkAndSyncLicenseIfNeeded",
                 "isLicenseCurrentlyValid", "licenseWatchdogTimerFired",
                 "A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease", "terminateApplicationWithBundleID:")
    assert all(word not in source for word in forbidden), "License/CarPlay runtime must NOT be restored"

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--dylib", required=True, type=pathlib.Path)
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=pathlib.Path)
    ap.add_argument("--report", required=True, type=pathlib.Path)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE.read_bytes()) == BASE_SHA, "Native16 published artifact mismatch"
    assert cmd("dpkg-deb", "-f", str(BASE), "Package") == "com.duoface.independent"
    assert cmd("dpkg-deb", "-f", str(BASE), "Version") == "0.1.0~native16"
    assert not args.out.exists() and not args.report.exists(), "Refuse overwrite of an existing build"
    assert args.dylib.is_file() and len(args.dylib.read_bytes()) > 2000, "Missing compiled UI module"
    source = pathlib.Path("runtime/settings/DFSettingsUIRestore.mm").read_text()
    check_source_only_ui(source)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native17-restore-ui-") as td:
        stage = pathlib.Path(td) / "stage"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb", "-R", str(BASE), str(stage)], check=True)
        before = inventory(stage)
        assert len(before) >= 43
        assert all(before.get(path) == checksum for path, checksum in EXPECTED_HOSTS.items())
        assert before.get(PREFIX + "DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib") == EXPECTED_SMART
        assert before.get(PREFIX + "DuoFaceInCarAddApps.dylib") == EXPECTED_CARPLAY
        assert not any("A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease" in p for p in before)
        assert MOD + ".dylib" not in before and MOD + ".plist" not in before
        shutil.copy2(args.dylib, stage / (MOD + ".dylib"))
        shutil.copy2(pathlib.Path("runtime/settings/DuoFaceSettingsUIRestore.plist"), stage / (MOD + ".plist"))
        subprocess.run(["ldid", "-S", str(stage / (MOD + ".dylib"))], check=True)
        subprocess.run(["lipo", str(stage / (MOD + ".dylib")), "-verify_arch", "arm64"], check=True)
        filt = plistlib.loads((stage / (MOD + ".plist")).read_bytes())["Filter"]
        assert filt == {"Executables": ["DuoFaceUI"], "Bundles": ["com.duoface.duofaceui"]}, filt
        after = inventory(stage)
        assert set(after) - set(before) == {MOD + ".dylib", MOD + ".plist"}
        assert not set(before) - set(after)
        assert all(after[k] == before[k] for k in before), "Existing native16 payload modified"
        control = stage / "DEBIAN/control"
        raw = control.read_text()
        assert re.search(r"(?m)^Version: 0\.1\.0~native16$", raw)
        control.write_text(re.sub(r"(?m)^Version: 0\.1\.0~native16$", "Version: " + VERSION, raw, count=1))
        checks = [hashlib.md5((stage / p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() + "  " + p
                  for p in sorted(after)]
        (stage / "DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(checks) + "\n")
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb", "--build", "--root-owner-group", str(stage), str(args.out)], check=True)
        assert cmd("dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Package") == "com.duoface.independent"
        assert cmd("dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Version") == VERSION
        assert cmd("dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Architecture") == "iphoneos-arm64"
        report = {
            "status": "NATIVE17_IOS_SETTINGS_UI_ONLY_NATIVE12_CORE_EXACT_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
            "package": "com.duoface.independent",
            "version": VERSION,
            "base_published_native16_sha256": BASE_SHA,
            "deb_sha256": sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
            "deb_bytes": args.out.stat().st_size,
            "preserved_native16_payload_entries": len(before),
            "new_payload_paths": [MOD + ".dylib", MOD + ".plist"],
            "all_native16_original_payload_byte_identical": True,
            "native12_exact_original_host_sha256": EXPECTED_HOSTS,
            "smartconnection_10s_unchanged_sha256": EXPECTED_SMART,
            "carplay_addapps_unchanged_sha256": EXPECTED_CARPLAY,
            "ui_module_sha256": after[MOD + ".dylib"],
            "ui_plist_sha256": after[MOD + ".plist"],
            "restored_license_methods_or_registration": False,
            "original_nolease_library_reintroduced": False,
            "dopamine_touched": False,
            "device_tested": False,
            "published_to_sileo": False,
            "limitations": "This restores native2-era SETTINGS ROW PRESENTATION only. It does NOT change the native12 license core, prove its runtime behavior, or verify row filtering on an iPhone.",
        }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native17_settings_ui_parity.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native18_direct_addapp.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild only direct AddApps module from published, hash-pinned native17.

The immutable source contains the native12 exact license core, native17
settings-only UI restoration and 10 second SmartConnection. No edits to those
files, original media, CarPlay menu layout or tweak filters.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile

BASE=Path("dist/payload-migration/native17/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native17_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
BASE_SHA="60f8a4277dd9df912036f93fc8edfd0897a516752f3014863459d5ce89ad5fff"
VERSION="0.1.0~native18"
PREF="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"
MODULE=PREF+"DuoFaceInCarAddApps.dylib"
DASH=PREF+"DuoFaceDashboard.dylib"
UI="var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI"
SMART=PREF+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib"
UI_RESTORE=PREF+"DuoFaceSettingsUIRestore.dylib"
REQUIRED_HASHES={
 UI:"cb40346d8ca504cb399abe31367847b4f980a01ba93810986ce1acf1fbe9eef9",
 DASH:"9e02a4bcee98fb703777122c67286158d9ca9cfa3088887b5ca280c580a695ea",
 SMART:"0986cf70b4c613ad011520204337e1e2b403d24111f39f14f70628fb57e61d50",
 UI_RESTORE:"680f79a1bb4d58a3bf277f7ed253f88627459390599a0818660f89dabe2e629f",
}
def sha(raw):return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
def check(*args):return subprocess.check_output(args,text=True,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).strip()
def entries(root):
 return {f.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(f.read_bytes()) for f in root.rglob("*")
         if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
 ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
 ap.add_argument("--dylib",required=True,type=Path)
 ap.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=Path)
 ap.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=Path)
 args=ap.parse_args()
 assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE.read_bytes())==BASE_SHA
 assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"
 assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Version")=="0.1.0~native17"
 assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
 assert args.dylib.is_file() and len(args.dylib.read_bytes())>3000
 assert not args.out.exists() and not args.report.exists(), "No overwrite of signed release"
 source=Path("runtime/appbridge/DFInCarAppPicker.xm").read_text()
 assert source.count("- (void)addMoreAppsTapped:(id)sender {") == 1
 assert "%orig" not in source, "Old modal fallback must never be reachable through new hook"
 assert 'dict[@"appSettings"]' in source and "kDFBridgedAppsKey" in source
 assert "DFInCarAppPicker" in source and "DFSaveToNativePreferences" in source
 assert "Dừng xe an toàn và mở DuoFace trên iPhone" not in source
 with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native18-app-picker-") as tmp:
  stage=Path(tmp)/"stage"
  subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-R",str(BASE),str(stage)],check=True)
  before=entries(stage)
  assert len(before)==45, len(before)
  assert all(before.get(k)==v for k,v in REQUIRED_HASHES.items())
  assert MODULE in before and PREF+"DuoFaceInCarAddApps.plist" in before
  assert not any("A_DuoFaceNativeNoLease" in x for x in before)
  shutil.copy2(args.dylib,stage/MODULE)
  subprocess.run(["ldid","-S",str(stage/MODULE)],check=True)
  subprocess.run(["lipo",str(stage/MODULE),"-verify_arch","arm64"],check=True)
  after=entries(stage)
  changed={p for p in before if before[p]!=after[p]}
  assert before.keys()==after.keys()
  assert changed=={MODULE},changed
  assert all(after[k]==v for k,v in REQUIRED_HASHES.items())
  filt=PREF+"DuoFaceInCarAddApps.plist"
  assert before[filt]==after[filt]
  control=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
  text=control.read_text()
  assert "Version: 0.1.0~native17" in text
  control.write_text(text.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native17","Version: "+VERSION,1))
  (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(
       hashlib.md5((stage/f).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+f for f in sorted(after))+"\n")
  args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(stage),str(args.out)],check=True)
  assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version")==VERSION
  assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"

   "status":"NATIVE18_DIRECT_CARPLAY_ADD_APP_NO_ORIGINAL_MODAL_COMPILED_CANDIDATE",

   "native18_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
   "native18_bytes":args.out.stat().st_size,

   "changed_payload_paths":sorted(changed),

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild only direct AddApps module from published, hash-pinned native18.

The immutable source contains the native12 exact license core, native18
settings-only UI restoration and 10 second SmartConnection. No edits to those
files, original media, CarPlay menu layout or tweak filters.

import hashlib
import json

import subprocess
import tempfile

BASE=Path("dist/payload-migration/native18/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native18_iphoneos-arm64.deb")

VERSION="0.1.0~native19"
PREF="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"

DASH=PREF+"DuoFaceDashboard.dylib"

 UI:"cb40346d8ca504cb399abe31367847b4f980a01ba93810986ce1acf1fbe9eef9",
 DASH:"9e02a4bcee98fb703777122c67286158d9ca9cfa3088887b5ca280c580a695ea",

 UI_RESTORE:"680f79a1bb4d58a3bf277f7ed253f88627459390599a0818660f89dabe2e629f",
}
def sha(raw):return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
def check(*args):return subprocess.check_output(args,text=True,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).strip()
def entries(root):
 return {f.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(f.read_bytes()) for f in root.rglob("*")
         if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def main():
 ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
 ap.add_argument("--dylib",required=True,type=Path)
 ap.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=Path)
 ap.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=Path)
 args=ap.parse_args()
 assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE.read_bytes())==BASE_SHA
 assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Package")=="com.duoface.independent"
 assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Version")=="0.1.0~native18"
 assert check("dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
 assert args.dylib.is_file() and len(args.dylib.read_bytes())>3000

 source=Path("runtime/appbridge/DFInCarAppPicker.xm").read_text()
 assert source.count("- (void)addMoreAppsTapped:(id)sender {") == 1

 with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native19-app-picker-") as tmp:

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Build native2 by REUSING proven native1 byte-for-byte staging and packaging.

Only the new 2-source add-on changes. The original DuoFaceUI, Dashboard,
FullscreenBridge, VisualHook, assets, and filters remain bit-identical.
"""
import argparse
import json
import pathlib

import build_native_trial as baseline

VERSION="0.1.0~native2"
NATIVE1_SHA256="16fd5fcbad7eba98de91a2e7a1200793509640cc47decbcca30491a45a6b9a13"

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--policy",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument("--out",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument("--report",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    args=p.parse_args()
    baseline.VERSION=VERSION
    baseline.main()
    report=json.loads(args.report.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert report["package"]=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert report["version"]==VERSION
    assert report["original_unchanged_payload_file_count"]>=37
    assert report["original_ui_source_rewritten"] is False
    report.update({
        "status":"ORIGINAL_UI_NATIVE2_CANDIDATE_BUILT_NEEDS_DEVICE_TEST",
        "based_on_proven_native1_sha256":NATIVE1_SHA256,
        "native2_changes":[
            "Suppress iPhone-only expiry modal entrypoint XXRootViewController.checkAndPresentLicenseModalIfNeeded",
            "Hide date/license/subscription rows from original CBGeneralSettingsViewController, preserving other row actions",
            "Disable obsolete subscription-navigation action from iPhone settings",
        ],
        "carplay_scene_host_changed":False,
        "carplay_picker_or_menu_changed":False,
        "original_source_binaries_changed":False,
        "original_license_bytecode_still_embedded":True,
        "original_time_value_hooks_preserved_from_working_native1":True,
        "rollback_original_native1_sha256":NATIVE1_SHA256,
        "device_tested":False,
        "sileo_published":False,
    })
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print("NATIVE2_UI_ONLY_TRIAL_PACKAGED; ORIGINAL_37_FILES_UNCHANGED; NEEDS_DEVICE_TEST")

if __name__=="__main__":main()
