########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native3_text.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verify original executable instructions remain identical in native3.

Rebranding changes only same-width user-facing strings and re-signs Mach-O.
This test hashes every __TEXT,__text section before and after, across slices.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile

SOURCE_NATIVE2="0d48d13e4c5965f8d787d86fa4c7776145cd843e98e7cae0b516779271264e60"
NATIVE2="dist/payload-migration/native2/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native2_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
NATIVE3="dist/payload-migration/native3/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native3_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
PATHS=[
    "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",
    "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib",
    "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceFullscreenBridge.dylib",
]
def sha(x:bytes):return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()
def slices(blob):
    magic=blob[:4]
    if magic in (bytes.fromhex("cafebabe"),bytes.fromhex("cafebabf")):
        count=struct.unpack_from(">I",blob,4)[0]
        wide=(magic==bytes.fromhex("cafebabf"))
        assert 1<=count<=8
        for i in range(count):
            pos=8+i*(32 if wide else 20)
            cpu,sub=struct.unpack_from(">II",blob,pos)
            off,size=struct.unpack_from(">QQ" if wide else ">II",blob,pos+8)
            assert off+size<=len(blob)
            yield f"{cpu:x}/{sub:x}",blob[off:off+size]
    else:
        cpu,sub=struct.unpack_from("<II",blob,4)
        yield f"{cpu:x}/{sub:x}",blob

def text_sections(blob:bytes):
    report={}
    for ident,part in slices(blob):
        assert part[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"),"Expected little endian 64-bit MachO"
        ncmds=struct.unpack_from("<I",part,16)[0]
        cursor=32
        for _ in range(ncmds):
            cmd,size=struct.unpack_from("<II",part,cursor)
            assert size>=8 and cursor+size<=len(part)
            if cmd==0x19:
                nsects=struct.unpack_from("<I",part,cursor+64)[0]
                for j in range(nsects):
                    section=cursor+72+j*80
                    assert section+80<=cursor+size
                    secname=part[section:section+16].split(b"\0")[0].decode("ascii")
                    segname=part[section+16:section+32].split(b"\0")[0].decode("ascii")
                    length=struct.unpack_from("<Q",part,section+40)[0]
                    file_offset=struct.unpack_from("<I",part,section+48)[0]
                    if secname=="__text" and segname in ("__TEXT","__TEXT_EXEC"):
                        assert length>512 and file_offset+length<=len(part)
                        report[f"{ident}:{segname}/{secname}"]={
                            "sha256":sha(part[file_offset:file_offset+length]),"size":length,
                        }
            cursor+=size
    assert report,"No MachO instruction segments"
    return report

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=Path)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    source=Path(NATIVE2);current=Path(NATIVE3)
    assert sha(source.read_bytes())==SOURCE_NATIVE2
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
        before=Path(d)/"native2"
        after=Path(d)/"native3"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(source),str(before)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(current),str(after)],check=True)
        originals={}
        for p in PATHS:
            orig=(before/p).read_bytes()
            revised=(after/p).read_bytes()
            old_sections=text_sections(orig)
            new_sections=text_sections(revised)
            assert old_sections==new_sections,f"Compiled executable instruction changed: {p}"
            originals[p]={"old_sha256":sha(orig),"new_sha256":sha(revised),
                          "old_file_bytes":len(orig),"new_file_bytes":len(revised),
                          "instruction_sections":old_sections}
        # The native2 runtime adapter and all original branding/images must
        # remain byte-identical. The build report enumerates intended changes.
        baseline_paths={}
        for p in before.rglob("*"):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink():
                baseline_paths[p.relative_to(before).as_posix()]=sha(p.read_bytes())
        current_paths={}
        for p in after.rglob("*"):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink():
                current_paths[p.relative_to(after).as_posix()]=sha(p.read_bytes())
        assert baseline_paths.keys()==current_paths.keys()
        diffs=sorted(k for k in baseline_paths if baseline_paths[k]!=current_paths[k])
        assert diffs==sorted(PATHS),diffs
    result={
       "status":"NATIVE3_MACHO_INSTRUCTIONS_IDENTICAL_AND_ALL_OTHER_DATA_FILES_BYTE_IDENTICAL",
       "changed_macho_files":originals,
       "unchanged_other_payload_file_count":len(baseline_paths)-len(PATHS),
       "instruction_sections_byte_identical":True,
       "device_tested":False,
       "note":"Byte-identical instructions do not prove unchanged runtime behavior where display strings are accessed; test real CarPlay.",
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print("NATIVE3_BYTE_IDENTICAL_MACHINE_INSTRUCTIONS_PASS",len(originals),
          "unchanged other files",result["unchanged_other_payload_file_count"])
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native3_text.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native4_regression.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independent regression check of native4 vs immutable native3.

Verify that EVERY executable byte outside an explicitly enumerated removed
method implementation stays identical and that each affected method was filled
with a same-ABI return stub and ARM64 NOP instructions. Original picker, assets,
rootless filter and native2 runtime adapter must remain unchanged.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile

from audit_native4_license_code import slurp_slices

ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
OLD=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native3/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native3_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
NEW=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native4/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native4_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
REP=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native4/BAO_CAO.json"
NOP=struct.pack("<I",0xd503201f)
RET=struct.pack("<I",0xd65f03c0)
BINS={
"DuoFaceUI":"var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",
"DuoFaceDashboard":"var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib",
}
def digest(x:bytes)->str:return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()

def executable_ranges(part:bytes):
    assert part[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe")
    out={}
    p=32
    n=struct.unpack_from("<I",part,16)[0]
    for _ in range(n):
        cmd,size=struct.unpack_from("<II",part,p)
        assert size>=8
        if cmd==0x19:
            num=struct.unpack_from("<I",part,p+64)[0]
            for i in range(num):
                q=p+72+i*80
                assert q+80<=p+size
                seg=part[q+16:q+32].split(b"\0")[0]
                sec=part[q:q+16].split(b"\0")[0]
                if seg in (b"__TEXT",b"__TEXT_EXEC") and sec==b"__text":
                    length=struct.unpack_from("<Q",part,q+40)[0]
                    off=struct.unpack_from("<I",part,q+48)[0]
                    assert length>1000 and off+length<=len(part)
                    out[(seg.decode(),sec.decode())]=(off,length)
        p+=size
    assert out
    return out

def verify_method_segments(before:bytes,after:bytes,methods:list[dict]):
    old_sections=executable_ranges(before)
    new_sections=executable_ranges(after)
    assert old_sections==new_sections,(old_sections,new_sections)
    whole_count=0
    replacement_bytes=0
    proof=[]
    already_inert=[]
    for key,(off,size) in old_sections.items():
        mask=bytearray(size)
        inside=[m for m in methods if off<=m["file_offset"]<off+size]
        for m in inside:
            start=m["file_offset"]-off
            end=start+m["bytes_replaced"]
            assert end<=size
            assert not any(mask[start:end]),"overlapping replaced span"
            mask[start:end]=b"\x01"*(end-start)
            original=before[m["file_offset"]:m["file_offset"]+m["bytes_replaced"]]
            replacement=after[m["file_offset"]:m["file_offset"]+m["bytes_replaced"]]
            assert digest(original)==m["previous_span_sha256"],m["selector"]
            stub_bytes=m["stub_bytes"]
            assert stub_bytes>=4 and stub_bytes%4==0
            assert replacement[stub_bytes-4:stub_bytes]==RET,(key,m["selector"],"missing ret")
            assert replacement[stub_bytes:]==NOP*((len(replacement)-stub_bytes)//4),(key,m["selector"],"not fully erased")
            if original==replacement:
                already_inert.append({'class':m['class'],'selector':m['selector'],'offset':m['file_offset'],'bytes':m['bytes_replaced']})
            replacement_bytes+=len(replacement)
            proof.append({"class":m["class"],"method":m["selector"],"span_sha256":digest(replacement)})
        a=before[off:off+size]
        b=after[off:off+size]
        assert len(a)==len(b)
        for i,flag in enumerate(mask):
            if not flag and a[i]!=b[i]:
                raise AssertionError(f"Original CarPlay machine byte unexpectedly changed: {key} {off+i:#x}")
            if not flag:whole_count+=1
    assert len(proof)==len(methods),(len(proof),len(methods))
    return {
      "verified_method_count":len(proof),
      "already_identical_in_baseline_method_count":len(already_inert),
      "already_identical_in_baseline_methods":already_inert,
      "fully_replaced_original_method_bytes":replacement_bytes,
      "unmodified_executable_bytes_outside_target_functions":whole_count,
      "all_other_executable_instruction_bytes_identical":True,
      "all_removed_functions_return_using_original_ABI_stubs":True,
      "methods_new_span_sha256":proof,
    }

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=pathlib.Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    report=json.loads(REP.read_text())
    assert digest(OLD.read_bytes())==report["baseline_native3_sha256"]
    assert digest(NEW.read_bytes())==report["deb_sha256"]
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as work:
        root=pathlib.Path(work)
        a=root/"a";b=root/"b"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(OLD),str(a)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(NEW),str(b)],check=True)
        all_old={p.relative_to(a).as_posix():digest(p.read_bytes())
                 for p in a.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        all_new={p.relative_to(b).as_posix():digest(p.read_bytes())
                 for p in b.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        assert all_old.keys()==all_new.keys(),"unexpected payload paths"
        actual_changed=sorted(k for k in all_old if all_old[k]!=all_new[k])
        assert actual_changed==sorted(BINS.values()),actual_changed
        evidence={}
        for name,path in BINS.items():
            original=(a/path).read_bytes()
            changed=(b/path).read_bytes()
            old=[x["bytes"] for x in slurp_slices(original) if x["arch"]=="arm64:0"]
            new=[x["bytes"] for x in slurp_slices(changed) if x["arch"]=="arm64:0"]
            assert len(old)==1 and len(new)==1
            methods=report["method_bodies"][name]["patched_methods"]
            evidence[name]=verify_method_segments(old[0],new[0],methods)
            assert evidence[name]["verified_method_count"]==report["method_bodies"][name]["method_body_count"]
            assert evidence[name]["fully_replaced_original_method_bytes"]==report["method_bodies"][name]["method_bytes_overwritten"]
    out={
        "status":"NATIVE4_ALL_NON_TARGET_MACHINE_INSTRUCTIONS_IDENTICAL",
        "source_native3_sha256":report["baseline_native3_sha256"],
        "native4_sha256":report["deb_sha256"],
        "original_payload_files_byte_identical_except_rewritten_binaries":len(all_old)-len(BINS),
        "correctly_modified_original_files":actual_changed,
        "executable_validation":evidence,
        "device_tested":False,
        "remaining_original_selectors_and_strings":True,
        "all_license_code_physically_removed":False,
        "note":"Even a rigorous executable diff cannot establish CarPlay behavior; this is an experimental DEB for test.",
    }
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps({"status":out["status"],"unchanged_payload_files":out["original_payload_files_byte_identical_except_rewritten_binaries"],
        "code_checks":{k:{"methods":v["verified_method_count"],"bytes":v["fully_replaced_original_method_bytes"],
                           "other_code_bytes_unchanged":v["unmodified_executable_bytes_outside_target_functions"]} for k,v in evidence.items()}},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native4_regression.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native5.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independent byte-level regression native4 -> native5.

Prove only two expired-modal methods can change __text, and that static strings
are masked ONLY where the build ledger explicitly records. No binary, icon,
picker, native hook, CarPlay resource or other payload may differ unexpectedly.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile
from audit_native4_license_code import inspect

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
BASE=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native4/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native4_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
CAND=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native5/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native5_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
REPORT=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native5/BAO_CAO.json"
LEDGER=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native5/DANH_SACH_CHO_XOA.json"
EXE={
 "DuoFaceUI":"var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",
 "DuoFaceDashboard":"var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib",
}
def sha(b:bytes)->str:return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def sections(blob:bytes):
    assert blob[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe")
    cursor=32
    n=struct.unpack_from("<I",blob,16)[0]
    result={}
    for _ in range(n):
        command,length=struct.unpack_from("<II",blob,cursor)
        assert length>=8 and cursor+length<=len(blob)
        if command==0x19:
            count=struct.unpack_from("<I",blob,cursor+64)[0]
            for i in range(count):
                q=cursor+72+i*80
                assert q+80<=cursor+length
                sec=blob[q:q+16].split(b"\x00")[0].decode("ascii")
                seg=blob[q+16:q+32].split(b"\x00")[0].decode("ascii")
                size=struct.unpack_from("<Q",blob,q+40)[0]
                offset=struct.unpack_from("<I",blob,q+48)[0]
                if size and offset+size<=len(blob):
                    result[f"{seg},{sec}"]=(offset,size)
        cursor+=length
    return result

def verify_edited_binary(binary,original,current,data):
    prior=sections(original)
    post=sections(current)
    expected=[];seen=[]
    masked=[]
    for m in data["masked_ui_literals"]:
        expected.append((m["file_offset"],m["byte_count"],"presentation_literal"))
        span=original[m["file_offset"]:m["file_offset"]+m["byte_count"]]
        revised=current[m["file_offset"]:m["file_offset"]+m["byte_count"]]
        assert sha(span)==m["before_sha256"]
        assert sha(revised)==m["after_sha256"]
        assert span!=revised
        assert all(x in (0x20,0x00) for x in revised)
        masked.append(m["label"])
    for m in data["method_flags"]:
        expected.append((m["file_offset"],m["byte_count"],"expiry_accessor"))
        span=original[m["file_offset"]:m["file_offset"]+m["byte_count"]]
        revised=current[m["file_offset"]:m["file_offset"]+m["byte_count"]]
        assert sha(span)==m["before_sha256"]
        assert sha(revised)==m["after_sha256"]
        assert span!=revised
    assert len(data["method_flags"])==(2 if binary=="DuoFaceUI" else 0)
    for part in ["__TEXT,__text","__TEXT_EXEC,__text","__TEXT,__cstring","__TEXT,__ustring"]:
        if part not in prior:continue
        assert prior[part]==post[part],(binary,part,"section offsets moved")
        off,length=prior[part]
        affected=[(start,start+size,kind) for start,size,kind in expected if off<=start<off+length]
        if part.endswith("__text"):
            assert all(kind=="expiry_accessor" for _,_,kind in affected)
        else:
            assert all(kind=="presentation_literal" for _,_,kind in affected)
        mask=bytearray(length)
        for start,end,kind in affected:
            assert end<=off+length
            a=start-off;b=end-off
            assert not any(mask[a:b]),"overlap"
            mask[a:b]=b"\x01"*(b-a)
            seen.append((start,end,kind))
        old=original[off:off+length]
        new=current[off:off+length]
        for i,(a,b) in enumerate(zip(old,new)):
            if not mask[i] and a!=b:
                raise AssertionError(f"Undeclared change in {binary} {part} at {hex(off+i)}")
    assert len(seen)==len(expected),(binary,len(seen),len(expected))
    # All Objective-C method definitions still available after reversible trial.
    a=inspect(original)["license_named_methods"]
    b=inspect(current)["license_named_methods"]
    assert sorted((m["class"],m["selector"],m["type_encoding"]) for m in a)==sorted(
           (m["class"],m["selector"],m["type_encoding"]) for m in b)
    return {"masked_presentation_literals":len(masked),
            "temporary_expiry_methods":len(data["method_flags"]),
            "verified_edited_byte_intervals":len(seen),
            "text_code_outside_expiry_methods_byte_identical":True,
            "objc_classes_and_selectors_preserved":True}

def main():
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    a.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    arg=a.parse_args()
    info=json.loads(REPORT.read_text())
    ledger=json.loads(LEDGER.read_text())
    assert sha(BASE.read_bytes())==info["source_native4_sha256"]
    assert sha(CAND.read_bytes())==info["deb_sha256"]
    assert len(ledger["method_hidden_records"])==2
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="native5-regression-") as temp:
        p=Path(temp);old=p/"old";new=p/"new"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(BASE),str(old)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(CAND),str(new)],check=True)
        ob={v.relative_to(old).as_posix():sha(v.read_bytes()) for v in old.rglob("*") if v.is_file() and not v.is_symlink()}
        nb={v.relative_to(new).as_posix():sha(v.read_bytes()) for v in new.rglob("*") if v.is_file() and not v.is_symlink()}
        assert ob.keys()==nb.keys()
        changed=sorted(k for k in ob if ob[k]!=nb[k])
        assert changed==sorted(EXE.values()),changed
        results={}
        for name,path in EXE.items():
            orig=(old/path).read_bytes()
            cand=(new/path).read_bytes()
            results[name]=verify_edited_binary(name,orig,cand,info["changed_binaries"][name])
    report={
        "status":"NATIVE5_NATIVE_CARPLAY_CODE_AND_UNRELATED_ASSETS_BYTE_IDENTICAL",
        "source_native4_sha256":info["source_native4_sha256"],
        "native5_sha256":info["deb_sha256"],
        "unchanged_all_other_payload_file_count":len(ob)-len(EXE),
        "target_binary_checks":results,
        "no_extra_machine_code_changed":True,
        "no_deletion_of_selectors_or_class_lifecycle":True,
        "device_tested":False,
        "approval_required_before_physical_delete":True,
    }
    arg.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    arg.out.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!="target_binary_checks"},ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native5.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native6.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independent native5 -> native6 byte-level regression of approved string erasure.

Assert every Mach-O section other than 26 zeroed display literal spans is exactly
equal to native5, including every instruction/selector/CarPlay lifecycle byte.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

from build_native5 import sections, FILES, sha
from build_native6 import SOURCE, SOURCE_HASH, LEDGER, VERSION

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]


def map_sections(buf):
    result = {}
    for seg, sec, off, size in sections(buf):
        key = (seg, sec)
        assert key not in result, ("duplicate section", key)
        result[key] = (off, size)
    return result


def verify_binary(name, before, after, entries, methods):
    assert len(before) == len(after), (name, "binary size changed")
    old_sections = map_sections(before)
    new_sections = map_sections(after)
    assert old_sections == new_sections, (name, "section moved")
    assert len(entries) == 13
    intervals = []
    for row in entries:
        start, length = int(row["file_offset"]), int(row["byte_count"])
        stop = start + length
        assert sha(before[start:stop]) == row["after_sha256"], (name, row["label"])
        assert after[start:stop] == bytes(length), (name, row["label"])
        assert sha(after[start:stop]) == hashlib.sha256(bytes(length)).hexdigest()
        matches = [
            k for k, (off, n) in old_sections.items()
            if off <= start and stop <= off + n
        ]
        assert len(matches) == 1 and ",".join(matches[0]) == row["section"]
        intervals.append((start, stop))
    intervals.sort()
    assert all(a[1] <= b[0] for a, b in zip(intervals, intervals[1:]))
    mask_count = 0
    changed_count = 0
    for key, (offset, size) in old_sections.items():
        original = before[offset:offset + size]
        current = after[offset:offset + size]
        mask = bytearray(size)
        for start, stop in intervals:
            if offset <= start and stop <= offset + size:
                mask[start - offset:stop - offset] = bytes([1]) * (stop - start)
                mask_count += 1
        for idx, (left, right) in enumerate(zip(original, current)):
            if left != right:
                assert mask[idx], (name, key, hex(offset + idx),
                                  "unapproved changed section byte")
                changed_count += 1
        if key[1] == "__text":
            assert original == current, (name, "machine code changed")
        if key[1] in {"__objc_classlist", "__objc_selrefs", "__objc_methname",
                      "__objc_classname", "__objc_const", "__objc_data"}:
            assert original == current, (name, key, "ObjC metadata changed")
    assert mask_count == len(entries), (name, mask_count)
    assert changed_count == sum(int(x["byte_count"]) for x in entries)
    if name == "DuoFaceUI":
        assert len(methods) == 2
        for m in methods:
            s = int(m["file_offset"])
            e = s + int(m["byte_count"])
            assert before[s:e] == after[s:e], ("expired ABI modified", m["selector"])
            assert sha(after[s:e]) == m["after_sha256"]
    return {"erased_literals": len(entries), "erased_bytes": changed_count,
            "all_machine_code_identical": True,
            "all_other_macho_sections_byte_identical": True,
            "objc_metadata_preserved": True}


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--deb", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--build-report", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    build = json.loads(args.build_report.read_text())
    ledger = json.loads(LEDGER.read_text())
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes()) == SOURCE_HASH
    assert sha(args.deb.read_bytes()) == build["deb_sha256"]
    assert build["version"] == VERSION and build["zero_erased_presentation_literals"] == 26
    assert len(ledger["ui_literal_hidden_records"]) == 26
    assert len(ledger["method_hidden_records"]) == 2
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="native6-check-") as temp:
        temp = Path(temp)
        old = temp / "native5"
        new = temp / "native6"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb", "-x", str(SOURCE), str(old)], check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb", "-x", str(args.deb), str(new)], check=True)
        orig = {x.relative_to(old).as_posix(): sha(x.read_bytes())
                for x in old.rglob("*") if x.is_file() and not x.is_symlink()}
        revised = {x.relative_to(new).as_posix(): sha(x.read_bytes())
                   for x in new.rglob("*") if x.is_file() and not x.is_symlink()}
        assert orig.keys() == revised.keys()
        changes = sorted(k for k in orig if orig[k] != revised[k])
        assert changes == sorted(FILES.values()), changes
        checks = {}
        for name, rel in FILES.items():
            entry = [x for x in ledger["ui_literal_hidden_records"]
                     if x["binary"] == name]
            methods = [x for x in ledger["method_hidden_records"]
                       if x["binary"] == name]
            checks[name] = verify_binary(
                name, (old / rel).read_bytes(), (new / rel).read_bytes(),
                entry, methods)
    info = {
        "status": "NATIVE6_EXACT_ZERO_ERASURE_BINARY_REGRESSION_PASS",
        "source_native5_sha256": SOURCE_HASH,
        "native6_sha256": build["deb_sha256"],
        "verified": checks,
        "untouched_payload_file_count": len(orig) - len(FILES),
        "methods_removed_physically": 0,
        "objc_classes_and_selectors_removed_physically": 0,
        "presentation_literal_bytes_physically_zeroed": sum(
            x["erased_bytes"] for x in checks.values()),
        "machine_code_changed": False,
        "license_business_logic_proven_fully_removed": False,
        "device_tested": False
    }
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(info, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps(info, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native6.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native7.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Strict read-only binary diff: native6 -> native7 can ONLY hide 78 strings.

All Mach-O instructions/ObjC metadata and every unrelated payload file must
remain byte-identical. Native7 remains a trial pending device confirmation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, json, subprocess, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from build_native5 import FILES, sections, sha
from build_native7 import SOURCE, EXPECTED, VERSION, UI_COPY

def verify_binary(name, old, new, masks):
    assert len(old)==len(new)
    original_sections={(seg,sec):(off,n) for seg,sec,off,n in sections(old)}
    modified_sections={(seg,sec):(off,n) for seg,sec,off,n in sections(new)}
    assert original_sections==modified_sections,(name,"Mach-O sections shifted")
    assert len(masks)==len(UI_COPY)==39
    intervals=set()
    for m in masks:
        start,length=m["file_offset"],m["byte_count"]
        stop=start+length
        assert m["binary"]==name
        assert sha(old[start:stop])==m["native6_sha256"]
        assert sha(new[start:stop])==m["native7_sha256"]
        assert new[start:stop]==b" "*length
        assert old[start:stop]==m["label"].encode("utf-8")
        assert (start,stop) not in intervals
        intervals.add((start,stop))
        assert any(sec=="__cstring" and off<=start<stop<=off+n
                   for (_,sec),(off,n) in original_sections.items())
    intervals=sorted(intervals)
    assert all(a[1]<=b[0] for a,b in zip(intervals,intervals[1:]))
    edited=0
    for (seg,sec),(off,size) in original_sections.items():
        left=old[off:off+size]
        right=new[off:off+size]
        spans=[(a-off,b-off) for a,b in intervals if off<=a and b<=off+size]
        assert (sec=="__cstring") or not spans
        for n,(x,y) in enumerate(zip(left,right)):
            if x!=y:
                assert sec=="__cstring" and any(a<=n<b for a,b in spans),(name,seg,sec,hex(off+n))
                edited+=1
        if sec=="__text":
            assert left==right,(name,"code modified")
        if sec.startswith("__objc") or sec=="__const" or sec=="__stubs":
            assert left==right,(name,sec,"ObjC runtime or ABI modified")
    assert edited==sum(m["byte_count"] for m in masks), (name,edited)
    return {"masked_strings":len(masks),"masked_bytes":edited,
            "machine_code_sections_identical":True,
            "all_other_sections_identical":True,
            "objc_runtime_metadata_identical":True}

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--deb",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    report=json.loads(args.report.read_text())
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes())==EXPECTED
    assert sha(args.deb.read_bytes())==report["deb_sha256"]
    assert report["version"]==VERSION
    assert len(report["masks"])==78
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native7-regression-") as folder:
        folder=Path(folder)
        a=folder/"native6"
        b=folder/"native7"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(SOURCE),str(a)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(args.deb),str(b)],check=True)
        old={p.relative_to(a).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
             for p in a.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        new={p.relative_to(b).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
             for p in b.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        assert old.keys()==new.keys()
        assert sorted(k for k in old if old[k]!=new[k])==sorted(FILES.values())
        tests={}
        for name,rel in FILES.items():
            tests[name]=verify_binary(name,(a/rel).read_bytes(),(b/rel).read_bytes(),
                [m for m in report["masks"] if m["binary"]==name])
    out={
      "status":"NATIVE7_ONLY_WHITELISTED_PRESENTATION_COPY_HIDDEN_PASS",
      "source_native6_sha256":EXPECTED,
      "native7_sha256":report["deb_sha256"],
      "verified_binaries":tests,
      "unrelated_payload_files_identical":len(old)-2,
      "all_carplay_code_identical":True,
      "objc_class_and_selector_metadata_preserved":True,
      "all_dealloc_and_carplay_detection_code_identical":True,
      "native7_device_tested":False,
      "license_logic_proven_fully_removed":False}
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(out,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native7.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native8.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Byte-level native6 -> native8 regression.

Only the audited 39 x 2 user-visible __cstring literals are masked in UI and
Dashboard Mach-O binaries, with machine instructions, lifecycle/CarPlay and
ObjC metadata identical. The source-built visual hook is the ONLY third
payload modification. Correctly counts characters actually changed (spaces
in the original text remain space, they are not changed bytes).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from build_native5 import FILES, sections, sha
from build_native7 import SOURCE, EXPECTED, UI_COPY
from build_native8 import VERSION, HOOK_REL, HOOK_FILTER_REL


def section_map(buf):
    d = {}
    for seg, sec, start, count in sections(buf):
        assert (seg, sec) not in d
        d[(seg, sec)] = (start, count)
    return d


def verify_display_binary(name, old, new, masks):
    assert len(old) == len(new), (name, "Mach-O size changed")
    src_sections = section_map(old)
    dst_sections = section_map(new)
    assert src_sections == dst_sections, (name, "Mach-O section shifted")
    assert len(masks) == len(UI_COPY) == 39
    assert {m["label"] for m in masks} == set(UI_COPY)

    spans = []
    for m in masks:
        a, size = int(m["file_offset"]), int(m["byte_count"])
        b = a + size
        assert m["binary"] == name
        assert old[a:b] == m["label"].encode("utf-8")
        assert new[a:b] == b" " * size
        assert sha(old[a:b]) == m["native6_sha256"]
        assert sha(new[a:b]) == m["native7_sha256"]
        assert old[b] == 0 and new[b] == 0, "C terminator must be retained"
        assert any(sec == "__cstring" and start <= a < b <= start + length
                   for (seg, sec), (start, length) in src_sections.items())
        spans.append((a,b))
    spans.sort()
    assert all(a[1] <= b[0] for a,b in zip(spans, spans[1:]))
    actual = 0
    for (seg, sec), (start, length) in src_sections.items():
        left = old[start:start+length]
        right = new[start:start+length]
        for i, (v, w) in enumerate(zip(left, right)):
            if v != w:
                assert sec == "__cstring", (name, seg, sec, hex(start+i))
                assert any(a <= start+i < b for a,b in spans), (name, hex(start+i))
                actual += 1
        if sec == "__text" or sec.startswith("__objc") or sec in ("__const", "__stubs"):
            assert left == right, (name, sec, "CarPlay/code/ObjC modified")
    expected = sum(
        sum(byte != 0x20 for byte in m["label"].encode("utf-8"))
        for m in masks
    )
    assert actual == expected, (name, actual, expected)
    return {
        "masked_user_visible_cstrings": len(masks),
        "selected_literal_bytes": sum(m["byte_count"] for m in masks),
        "actual_changed_literal_bytes": actual,
        "machine_code_unchanged": True,
        "objc_class_selector_metadata_unchanged": True,
        "carplay_lifecycle_and_detectors_unchanged": True,
    }


def verify():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--deb", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    report = json.loads(args.report.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes()) == EXPECTED, "Pinned native6 baseline changed"
    assert sha(args.deb.read_bytes()) == report["deb_sha256"]
    assert report["version"] == VERSION
    assert report["package"] == "com.duoface.independent"
    assert len(report["masks"]) == 78
    assert report["hold_duration_seconds"] == 0.30
    assert report["replaced_hook"] == HOOK_REL
    assert report["license_ui_strings_selected"] == 39
    assert report["license_ui_literal_occurrences_hidden"] == 78
    assert report["native6_rollback_preserved"]
    assert report["license_full_removal_claimed"] is False

    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.deb),"Package"],text=True
    ).strip() == "com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.deb),"Version"],text=True
    ).strip() == VERSION
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.deb),"Architecture"],text=True
    ).strip() == "iphoneos-arm64"

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native8-QA-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        src = root / "native6"
        out = root / "native8"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(SOURCE),str(src)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(args.deb),str(out)],check=True)
        before = {p.relative_to(src).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
                  for p in src.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        after = {p.relative_to(out).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
                 for p in out.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        assert before.keys() == after.keys(), "Added/dropped payload"
        allowed = sorted([*FILES.values(), HOOK_REL])
        changed = sorted(k for k in before if before[k] != after[k])
        assert changed == allowed, (changed, allowed)
        checks = {}
        for name, rel in FILES.items():
            checks[name] = verify_display_binary(
                name,
                (src/rel).read_bytes(),
                (out/rel).read_bytes(),
                [m for m in report["masks"] if m["binary"] == name]
            )
        hook_before = (src/HOOK_REL).read_bytes()
        hook_after = (out/HOOK_REL).read_bytes()
        assert sha(hook_before) == report["original_hook_sha256"]
        assert sha(hook_after) == report["new_hook_sha256"]
        assert hook_before != hook_after
        assert (src/HOOK_FILTER_REL).read_bytes() == (out/HOOK_FILTER_REL).read_bytes()
        hook_strings = subprocess.run(
            ["strings",str(out/HOOK_REL)],check=True,text=True,capture_output=True
        ).stdout
        for marker in ("DuoFaceGrip", "threshold=0.30s", "onSwap",
                       "DFDividerLayout"):
            # DFDividerLayout may be a local static symbol stripped by toolchain;
            # use explicit logged markers to verify binary behavior if needed.
            if marker == "DFDividerLayout":
                assert "divider native tap preserved" in hook_strings, "Lost divider hook"
            else:
                assert marker in hook_strings, (marker, "missing in source-built hook")
        # Baseline has extra DEBIAN control files excluded by dpkg-deb -x;
        # do not assume a 40-file data payload. Assert actual invariants.
        assert len(before) >= len(allowed) + 30
        assert len(before) == len(after)
        assert sum(x["actual_changed_literal_bytes"] for x in checks.values()) == (
            report["actual_changed_literal_bytes_expected"])
        assert sum(x["selected_literal_bytes"] for x in checks.values()) == (
            report["license_ui_selected_bytes"])

    result = {
        "status": "NATIVE8_CARPLAY_CODE_IDENTICAL_AND_GRIP_HOOK_DELTA_PASS",
        "native6_baseline_sha256": EXPECTED,
        "native8_sha256": report["deb_sha256"],
        "package": report["package"],
        "version": VERSION,
        "verified_binary_display_mappings": checks,
        "only_hook_and_two_binary_literal_data_changed": True,
        "identical_other_payload_files": len(before)-len(allowed),
        "unchanged_carplay_split_code_and_lifecycle": True,
        "unchanged_objc_selectors_classes_keys": True,
        "logo_grip_hook_verified_present": True,
        "logo_long_hold_threshold_seconds": 0.30,
        "rootless_architecture": "iphoneos-arm64",
        "device_tested": False,
        "sileo_published": False,
        "license_subsystem_fully_removed": False,
    }
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",
                        encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native8.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native9.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Enforce native9 release safety: original visuals + native gestures.

Uses proven native6 (SHA-pinned) as the only reference, verifies all byte
changes to two __cstring sections, original fullscreen/UI/visual dylib and
filter unchanged, and precisely two added signed logo-grip files.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import plistlib
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from build_native5 import FILES, sha
from build_native7 import SOURCE, EXPECTED, UI_COPY
from build_native9 import VERSION, BASE_VISUAL, BASE_FILTER, GRIP_DYLIB, GRIP_FILTER, ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256
from verify_native8 import verify_display_binary

def digest_files(root):
    return {
        p.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
        for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()
    }

def validate():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--deb",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes())==EXPECTED
    report=json.loads(args.report.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert report["status"]=="NATIVE9_ORIGINAL_VISUAL_PLUS_GRIP_ONLY_STAGED_NEEDS_CARPLAY_DEVICE_QA"
    assert report["native6_base_sha256"]==EXPECTED
    assert report["deb_sha256"]==sha(args.deb.read_bytes())
    assert report["package"]=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert report["version"]==VERSION
    assert report["architecture"]=="iphoneos-arm64"
    assert report["logo_hold_duration_seconds"]==0.30
    assert len(report["masks"])==78
    assert report["license_labels_selected"]==39
    assert report["license_copy_occurrences_hidden"]==78
    assert report["native_divider_pan_hooked"] is False
    assert report["fullscreen_legacy_visual_hook_replaced"] is False
    assert report["source_native6_rollback_preserved"]
    assert report["native8_rollback_preserved"]
    assert not report["device_tested"] and not report["sileo_published"]
    assert report["original_visual_sha256_unchanged"]==ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.deb),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.deb),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.deb),"Architecture"],text=True).strip()=="iphoneos-arm64"

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native9-verify-") as td:
        p=Path(td)
        base=p/"base"
        candidate=p/"candidate"
        candidate_control=p/"control"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(SOURCE),str(base)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-x",str(args.deb),str(candidate)],check=True)
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb","-e",str(args.deb),str(candidate_control)],check=True)
        prev=digest_files(base)
        after=digest_files(candidate)
        assert set(prev)<=set(after)
        assert set(after)-set(prev)=={GRIP_DYLIB,GRIP_FILTER}
        modified=sorted(k for k in prev if prev[k]!=after[k])
        assert modified==sorted(FILES.values()),modified
        assert sorted(report["changed_existing_paths"])==sorted([*FILES.values(),"DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"])
        assert report["added_paths"]==sorted([GRIP_DYLIB,GRIP_FILTER])
        assert prev[BASE_VISUAL]==after[BASE_VISUAL]==ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256
        assert prev[BASE_FILTER]==after[BASE_FILTER]==report["original_visual_filter_sha256_unchanged"]
        assert after[GRIP_FILTER]==prev[BASE_FILTER]==report["new_grip_filter_sha256"]
        original_filter=plistlib.loads((base/BASE_FILTER).read_bytes())
        independent_filter=plistlib.loads((candidate/GRIP_FILTER).read_bytes())
        assert original_filter==independent_filter
        assert "Bundles" in independent_filter["Filter"]
        assert len(independent_filter["Filter"]["Bundles"])>0
        assert after[GRIP_DYLIB]==report["new_grip_sha256"]
        assert (candidate/GRIP_DYLIB).stat().st_size>10000
        assert (candidate/GRIP_DYLIB).read_bytes()[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe")
        assert (base/GRIP_DYLIB).exists() is False
        assert (base/GRIP_FILTER).exists() is False

        checks={}
        for name,rel in FILES.items():
            checks[name]=verify_display_binary(
                name,(base/rel).read_bytes(),(candidate/rel).read_bytes(),
                [item for item in report["masks"] if item["binary"]==name])
        assert sum(x["masked_user_visible_cstrings"] for x in checks.values())==78
        assert sum(x["actual_changed_literal_bytes"] for x in checks.values())==report["actual_changed_literal_bytes_expected"]
        assert {item["label"] for item in report["masks"]}==set(UI_COPY)

        # Regenerated DEBIAN/md5sums must describe every actual payload file.
        sums=(candidate_control/"md5sums").read_text().splitlines()
        entries={}
        for line in sums:
            hexdigest,rel=line.split("  ",1)
            assert rel not in entries
            entries[rel]=hexdigest
        assert set(entries)==set(after)
        for rel,expected in entries.items():
            assert hashlib.md5((candidate/rel).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==expected,rel

        strings=subprocess.check_output(["strings",str(candidate/GRIP_DYLIB)],text=True)
        assert "[DuoFaceGripOnly] installed:" in strings
        assert "threshold=0.30s" in strings
        assert "native divider pan" in strings
        assert "DFDividerLayout" not in strings or "DuoFaceGripOnly" in strings
        control=(candidate_control/"control").read_text()
        assert "Package: com.duoface.independent" in control
        assert "Version: "+VERSION in control

        result={
            "status":"NATIVE9_BYTE_EXACT_ORIGINAL_VISUAL_AND_NATIVE_RATIO_GUARD_PASS",
            "native6_reference_sha256":EXPECTED,
            "native9_sha256":report["deb_sha256"],
            "package":report["package"],"version":VERSION,
            "rootless_architecture":report["architecture"],
            "native6_original_visual_dylib_byte_identical":True,
            "original_visual_dylib_sha256":ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256,
            "original_visual_filter_byte_identical":True,
            "grip_filter_exact_copy_of_original":True,
            "new_grip_dylib_sha256":after[GRIP_DYLIB],
            "only_added_paths":sorted([GRIP_DYLIB,GRIP_FILTER]),
            "only_changed_existing_payload":modified,
            "unchanged_other_existing_payload":len(prev)-len(modified),
            "license_copy_mask_checks":checks,
            "native_host_code_and_objc_metadata_unchanged":True,
            "legacy_ui_and_youtube_fullscreen_binary_untouched":True,
            "native_divider_pan_unhooked_by_new_module":True,
            "logo_hold_seconds":0.30,
            "md5sums_verified_all_payload":True,
            "not_claiming_device_success":True,
            "device_tested_for_this_candidate":False,
            "sileo_deployed":False
        }
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__":
    validate()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/verify_native9.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: runtime/appbridge/DFInCarAppPicker.xm ##########
// DuoFace CarPlay direct Add App — source fix after native15 device QA.
// Keep the native Add App tile, but NEVER call the old stop-car/iPhone-only
// modal. Open a CarPlay picker for valid choices or show a scoped in-car
// explanation when native storage/providers cannot yet be validated.
// This change never patches the native12 Dashboard/activation implementation.
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>
#import <UIKit/UIKit.h>
#import <objc/runtime.h>

@interface NSObject (DFInstalledAppInfo)
+ (id)defaultWorkspace;
- (NSArray *)allInstalledApplications;
- (NSString *)applicationIdentifier;
- (NSString *)localizedName;
- (NSString *)applicationType;
@end

@interface CBDashboardSettings : NSObject
+ (instancetype)sharedInstance;
- (NSArray<NSString *> *)bridgedApps;
- (void)reload;
@end

@interface CBBridgeManager : NSObject
+ (instancetype)sharedInstanceIfExists;
- (void)handleSettingsChanged;
@end

@interface CBMultitaskingOverlayView : UIView
- (void)addMoreAppsTapped:(id)sender;
- (void)switchToAppPickerMode;
@end

static const NSInteger kDFInCarModalTag=0x0DFA0023;
static NSString *const kDFBridgedAppsKey=@"bridgedApps";
static NSString *const kDFNativeOwnerPrefsFile=@"/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.duoface.plist";
static BOOL DFPickerValidBundle(NSString *bid) {
    if (![bid isKindOfClass:NSString.class] || bid.length<3 || bid.length>255 ||
        ![bid containsString:@"."] || [bid containsString:@".."]) return NO;
    NSCharacterSet *allow=[NSCharacterSet characterSetWithCharactersInString:
        @"abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789.-_"];
    return [bid rangeOfCharacterFromSet:allow.invertedSet].location==NSNotFound;
}

@interface UIImage (DFPrivateInstalledAppIcon)
+ (UIImage *)_applicationIconImageForBundleIdentifier:(NSString *)identifier
                                               format:(int)format scale:(CGFloat)scale;
@end

// SpringBoard is the native icon owner. Avoid loading every icon through a
// separate process or treating the bundle identifier as an asset pathname.
// Apple private selector may vary by iOS; show a safe symbol fallback.
static UIImage *DFInCarIconForBundle(NSString *bundleID) {
    if (!DFPickerValidBundle(bundleID)) return nil;
    static NSCache<NSString *, UIImage *> *cached=nil;
    static dispatch_once_t once;
    dispatch_once(&once, ^{ cached=[NSCache new]; cached.countLimit=140; });
    UIImage *hit=[cached objectForKey:bundleID];
    if (hit) return hit;
    UIImage *icon=nil;
    SEL selector=@selector(_applicationIconImageForBundleIdentifier:format:scale:);
    if ([UIImage respondsToSelector:selector]) {
        @try {
            icon=[UIImage _applicationIconImageForBundleIdentifier:bundleID
                              format:2 scale:UIScreen.mainScreen.scale];
        } @catch (NSException *ex) {
            NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] icon provider rejected app (%@)",ex.name);
        }
    }
    if (![icon isKindOfClass:UIImage.class] || !icon.size.width || !icon.size.height)
        icon=[UIImage systemImageNamed:@"app.fill"];
    if (icon) [cached setObject:icon forKey:bundleID];
    return icon;
}

static NSArray<NSString *> *DFValidStringApps(id value) {
    if (![value isKindOfClass:NSArray.class]) return nil;
    NSMutableArray<NSString *> *result=[NSMutableArray array];
    NSMutableSet<NSString *> *seen=[NSMutableSet set];
    for (id item in value) {
        if (!DFPickerValidBundle(item)) return nil;
        if ([seen containsObject:item]) return nil;
        [seen addObject:item]; [result addObject:item];
    }
    return [result copy];
}
static BOOL DFListEqual(NSArray *a,NSArray *b) {
    // Original preference array order is meaningful: never sort or replace it.
    return a && b && [a isEqualToArray:b];
}
static NSString *DFExistingSettingsFileForList(NSArray<NSString *> *current) {
    // Native20 rebuild patches the original native12 owner decoder to this
    // same canonical DuoFace preference file. Package preinst migrates existing
    // settings once, without recreating the app list from memory.
    if (!DFValidStringApps(current)) return nil;
    NSDictionary *dict=[NSDictionary dictionaryWithContentsOfFile:kDFNativeOwnerPrefsFile];
    if (![dict isKindOfClass:NSDictionary.class]) {
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] native settings owner not readable");
        return nil;
    }
    // appSettings is optional if no per-app configuration has been saved.
    id extra=dict[@"appSettings"];
    if (extra && ![extra isKindOfClass:NSDictionary.class]) {
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] invalid appSettings schema");
        return nil;
    }
    NSArray *saved=DFValidStringApps(dict[kDFBridgedAppsKey]);
    if (!DFListEqual(saved,current)) {
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] native preference owner list differs");
        return nil;
    }
    return kDFNativeOwnerPrefsFile;
}
static NSArray<NSDictionary *> *DFEligibleApps(NSArray<NSString *> *already) {
    Class cls=NSClassFromString(@"LSApplicationWorkspace");
    if (!cls || ![cls respondsToSelector:@selector(defaultWorkspace)]) return nil;
    id work=[cls defaultWorkspace];
    if (!work || ![work respondsToSelector:@selector(allInstalledApplications)])
        return nil;
    NSArray *installed=[work allInstalledApplications];
    if (![installed isKindOfClass:NSArray.class]) return nil;
    NSSet *selected=[NSSet setWithArray:already];
    NSMutableDictionary<NSString *,NSDictionary *> *unique=[NSMutableDictionary dictionary];
    for (id proxy in installed) {
        if (![proxy respondsToSelector:@selector(applicationIdentifier)] ||
            ![proxy respondsToSelector:@selector(localizedName)]) continue;
        NSString *bid=[proxy applicationIdentifier];
        NSString *name=[proxy localizedName];
        if (!DFPickerValidBundle(bid) || [selected containsObject:bid] ||
            ![name isKindOfClass:NSString.class] || !name.length ||
            [bid hasPrefix:@"com.apple."] || [bid hasPrefix:@"com.duoface."]) continue;
        if ([proxy respondsToSelector:@selector(applicationType)]) {
            id kind=[proxy applicationType];
            if ([kind isKindOfClass:NSString.class] && ![kind isEqualToString:@"User"])
                continue;
        }
        unique[bid]=@{@"bundle":bid,@"title":name};
    }
    return [[unique allValues] sortedArrayUsingComparator:
        ^NSComparisonResult(NSDictionary *a,NSDictionary *b) {
            NSComparisonResult x=[a[@"title"] localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare:b[@"title"]];
            return x==NSOrderedSame ? [a[@"bundle"] compare:b[@"bundle"]] : x;
        }];
}
static BOOL DFSaveToNativePreferences(NSString *file,NSArray *expected,
                                     NSString *bundle,NSString **failure) {
    if (!file.length || !DFPickerValidBundle(bundle)) {
        if (failure) *failure=@"Không tìm thấy cấu hình ứng dụng gốc.";
        return NO;
    }
    NSDictionary *before=[NSDictionary dictionaryWithContentsOfFile:file];
    NSArray *live=DFValidStringApps(before[kDFBridgedAppsKey]);
    if (!DFListEqual(live,expected)) {
        if (failure) *failure=@"Danh sách vừa thay đổi, vui lòng mở lại.";
        return NO;
    }
    if ([live containsObject:bundle]) return YES; // idempotent
    NSMutableDictionary *changed=[before mutableCopy];
    NSMutableArray *next=[live mutableCopy];
    [next addObject:bundle];
    changed[kDFBridgedAppsKey]=[next copy];
    if (![changed writeToFile:file atomically:YES]) {
        if (failure) *failure=@"Không thể lưu cấu hình trên iPhone.";
        return NO;
    }
    CBDashboardSettings *settings=[NSClassFromString(@"CBDashboardSettings") sharedInstance];
    if (![settings respondsToSelector:@selector(reload)]) {
        [before writeToFile:file atomically:YES];
        if (failure) *failure=@"Không tìm thấy bộ quản lý DuoFace.";
        return NO;
    }
    [settings reload];
    NSArray *updated=DFValidStringApps([settings bridgedApps]);
    if (!DFListEqual(updated,next)) {
        [before writeToFile:file atomically:YES];
        [settings reload]; // rollback persistent+in-memory state together
        if (failure) *failure=@"DuoFace không nhận danh sách mới; đã hoàn tác.";
        return NO;
    }
    CBBridgeManager *manager=[NSClassFromString(@"CBBridgeManager") sharedInstanceIfExists];
    if ([manager respondsToSelector:@selector(handleSettingsChanged)])
        [manager handleSettingsChanged];
    return YES;
}
@interface DFInCarAppPicker : UIView <UITableViewDataSource,UITableViewDelegate>
@property(nonatomic,weak) CBMultitaskingOverlayView *host;
@property(nonatomic,copy) NSString *prefsFile;
@property(nonatomic,copy) NSArray<NSString *> *snapshot;
@property(nonatomic,copy) NSArray<NSDictionary *> *choices;
@property(nonatomic,strong) UITableView *table;
@property(nonatomic,strong) UILabel *status;
- (instancetype)initWithHost:(CBMultitaskingOverlayView *)host
                       file:(NSString *)file current:(NSArray *)current
                    choices:(NSArray *)choices diagnostic:(NSString *)diagnostic;
- (void)dismiss;
@end
@implementation DFInCarAppPicker
- (instancetype)initWithHost:(CBMultitaskingOverlayView *)host
                       file:(NSString *)file current:(NSArray *)current
                    choices:(NSArray *)choices diagnostic:(NSString *)diagnostic {
    self=[super initWithFrame:host.bounds];
    if (!self) return nil;
    _host=host;_prefsFile=[file copy];_snapshot=[current copy];_choices=[choices copy];
    self.tag=kDFInCarModalTag;
    self.autoresizingMask=UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleWidth|UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleHeight;
    self.backgroundColor=[[UIColor blackColor] colorWithAlphaComponent:0.82];
    UIView *panel=[[UIView alloc] initWithFrame:CGRectInset(self.bounds,18,18)];
    panel.autoresizingMask=UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleWidth|UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleHeight;
    panel.layer.cornerRadius=14;
    panel.layer.masksToBounds=YES;
    panel.backgroundColor=[UIColor colorWithWhite:0.12 alpha:0.99];
    [self addSubview:panel];
    UIButton *cancel=[UIButton buttonWithType:UIButtonTypeSystem];
    [cancel setTitle:@"Hủy" forState:UIControlStateNormal];
    cancel.frame=CGRectMake(8,4,64,40);
    [cancel addTarget:self action:@selector(dismiss)
      forControlEvents:UIControlEventTouchUpInside];
    [panel addSubview:cancel];
    UILabel *title=[[UILabel alloc] initWithFrame:CGRectMake(76,4,
                            MAX(80,panel.bounds.size.width-154),40)];
    title.autoresizingMask=UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleWidth;
    title.textAlignment=NSTextAlignmentCenter;
    title.textColor=UIColor.whiteColor;
    title.font=[UIFont boldSystemFontOfSize:16];
    title.text=@"Thêm ứng dụng";
    [panel addSubview:title];
    UILabel *status=[[UILabel alloc] initWithFrame:CGRectMake(10,
                      panel.bounds.size.height-34,panel.bounds.size.width-20,28)];
    status.autoresizingMask=UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleWidth|UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleTopMargin;
    status.font=[UIFont systemFontOfSize:12];
    status.numberOfLines=2;
    status.adjustsFontSizeToFitWidth=YES;
    status.textColor=UIColor.lightGrayColor;
    status.textAlignment=NSTextAlignmentCenter;
    status.text=diagnostic.length ? diagnostic : @"Chạm để thêm vào DuoFace";
    [panel addSubview:status];_status=status;
    UITableView *table=[[UITableView alloc] initWithFrame:CGRectMake(0,46,
                      panel.bounds.size.width,MAX(55,panel.bounds.size.height-82))
                      style:UITableViewStylePlain];
    table.autoresizingMask=UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleWidth|UIViewAutoresizingFlexibleHeight;
    table.dataSource=self;table.delegate=self;table.rowHeight=50;
    table.backgroundColor=UIColor.clearColor;
    [panel addSubview:table];_table=table;
    return self;
}
- (NSInteger)tableView:(UITableView *)tableView numberOfRowsInSection:(NSInteger)section {
    (void)tableView;(void)section;return self.choices.count;
}
- (UITableViewCell *)tableView:(UITableView *)tableView
 cellForRowAtIndexPath:(NSIndexPath *)indexPath {
    static NSString *ident=@"DFInCarInstalledApp";
    UITableViewCell *cell=[tableView dequeueReusableCellWithIdentifier:ident];
    if (!cell) cell=[[UITableViewCell alloc] initWithStyle:UITableViewCellStyleSubtitle
                                            reuseIdentifier:ident];
    NSDictionary *entry=self.choices[(NSUInteger)indexPath.row];
    cell.textLabel.text=entry[@"title"];
    cell.textLabel.font=[UIFont systemFontOfSize:14];
    cell.textLabel.textColor=UIColor.whiteColor;
    cell.detailTextLabel.text=entry[@"bundle"];
    cell.detailTextLabel.textColor=UIColor.lightGrayColor;
    cell.detailTextLabel.font=[UIFont systemFontOfSize:10];
    UIImage *appIcon=DFInCarIconForBundle(entry[@"bundle"]);
    cell.imageView.image=appIcon;
    cell.imageView.contentMode=UIViewContentModeScaleAspectFit;
    cell.imageView.layer.cornerRadius=8;
    cell.imageView.layer.masksToBounds=YES;
    cell.backgroundColor=UIColor.clearColor;
    cell.accessoryType=UITableViewCellAccessoryDisclosureIndicator;
    return cell;
}
- (void)tableView:(UITableView *)tableView didSelectRowAtIndexPath:(NSIndexPath *)indexPath {
    [tableView deselectRowAtIndexPath:indexPath animated:YES];
    if ((NSUInteger)indexPath.row>=self.choices.count) return;
    if (!self.prefsFile.length || !self.snapshot) {
        self.status.text=@"Chưa xác minh được tệp cấu hình gốc; không ghi dữ liệu.";
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] save skipped: original preferences owner unverified");
        return;
    }
    NSString *bundle=self.choices[(NSUInteger)indexPath.row][@"bundle"];
    NSString *error=nil;
    BOOL ok=DFSaveToNativePreferences(self.prefsFile,self.snapshot,bundle,&error);
    if (!ok) {self.status.text=error?:@"Không thể thêm ứng dụng.";return;}
    NSLog(@"[DuoFace] CarPlay Add App saved (bundle-id redacted)");
    [self dismiss];
    // Original native picker owns its app cards, hit areas and split layout.
    // A reload of its original app-picker mode makes the new app selectable.
    [self.host switchToAppPickerMode];
}
- (void)dismiss {
    [self removeFromSuperview];
}
@end

%group DFInCarPicker
%hook CBMultitaskingOverlayView
- (void)addMoreAppsTapped:(id)sender {
    // THE ORIGINAL HANDLER SHOWS THE OBSOLETE "PARK SAFELY / OPEN IPHONE"
    // MODAL. It MUST NOT be invoked here on any success or failure branch.
    if (![NSThread isMainThread]) {
        dispatch_async(dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
            [self addMoreAppsTapped:sender];
        });
        return;
    }
    if ([self viewWithTag:kDFInCarModalTag]) return;

    Class settingsClass=NSClassFromString(@"CBDashboardSettings");
    CBDashboardSettings *settings=nil;
    if (settingsClass && [settingsClass respondsToSelector:@selector(sharedInstance)])
        settings=[settingsClass sharedInstance];
    NSArray *selected=settings ? DFValidStringApps([settings bridgedApps]) : nil;
    NSString *prefs=selected ? DFExistingSettingsFileForList(selected) : nil;
    NSArray *choices=DFEligibleApps(selected?:@[]);
    NSString *diagnostic=nil;
    if (!settings || !selected) {
        diagnostic=@"Chưa đọc được danh sách DuoFace gốc trên CarPlay.";
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] in-car status: settings/current list unavailable");
    } else if (!prefs) {
        diagnostic=@"Chưa xác minh tệp lưu chung với iPhone; chưa thể thêm.";
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] in-car status: shared settings file missing or ambiguous");
    } else if (!choices) {
        diagnostic=@"Chưa lấy được danh sách ứng dụng iPhone.";
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] in-car status: installed apps provider unavailable");
    } else if (choices.count==0) {
        diagnostic=@"Không còn ứng dụng phù hợp để thêm.";
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] in-car status: no eligible unbridged apps");
    }
    DFInCarAppPicker *picker=[[DFInCarAppPicker alloc]
        initWithHost:self file:prefs current:selected
        choices:choices?:@[] diagnostic:diagnostic];
    [self addSubview:picker];
    [self bringSubviewToFront:picker];
    NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] in-car sheet shown; choices=%lu verified-storage=%@",
          (unsigned long)choices.count, prefs?@"yes":@"no");
}
%end
%end

static BOOL DFPickerHookInstalled=NO;
static BOOL DFPickerAllowedHost(void) {
    NSString *bundle=NSBundle.mainBundle.bundleIdentifier.lowercaseString;
    NSString *process=NSProcessInfo.processInfo.processName.lowercaseString;
    NSSet *bundleAllow=[NSSet setWithArray:@[
        @"com.apple.springboard",@"com.apple.carplay",@"com.apple.carplayapp",
        @"com.apple.dashboard"]];
    NSSet *processAllow=[NSSet setWithArray:@[
        @"springboard",@"carplay",@"carplayapp",@"dashboard"]];
    return [bundleAllow containsObject:bundle] || [processAllow containsObject:process];
}
static void DFInstallPickerOnce(void) {
    if (DFPickerHookInstalled || !DFPickerAllowedHost()) return;
    // DuoFaceDashboard may be injected only once CarPlay is activated. A
    // one-time SpringBoard constructor probe silently missed this class.
    if (!NSClassFromString(@"CBMultitaskingOverlayView")) return;
    %init(DFInCarPicker);
    DFPickerHookInstalled=YES;
    NSLog(@"[DuoFaceInCarAddApps] Hook active in CarPlay host.");
}
static void DFInstallPickerEventually(void) {
    if (DFPickerHookInstalled || !DFPickerAllowedHost()) return;
    DFInstallPickerOnce();
    if (!DFPickerHookInstalled) {
        // Only one pending low-frequency probe; harmless if CarPlay attaches
        // much later after device boot. Stop immediately after %init succeeds.
        dispatch_after(dispatch_time(DISPATCH_TIME_NOW, 5*NSEC_PER_SEC),
                       dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
            DFInstallPickerEventually();
        });
    }
}
__attribute__((constructor)) static void DFInstallDirectAddApp(void) {
    @autoreleasepool {
        if (!DFPickerAllowedHost()) return;
        dispatch_async(dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
            DFInstallPickerEventually();
        });
    }
}
########## END FILE: runtime/appbridge/DFInCarAppPicker.xm ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: runtime/appbridge/DFRemoveOldAddAppTile.xm ##########
// native21: remove the failed CarPlay "Add App" tile, not the iPhone picker.
// No app-list writes, no alert, no bridge/geometry/zoom modifications.
#import <UIKit/UIKit.h>
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>

@interface CBMultitaskingOverlayView : UIView
- (void)addMoreAppsTapped:(id)sender;
- (void)setupAppPickerCardsInCardW:(CGFloat)w cardH:(CGFloat)h contentY:(CGFloat)y;
@end

static BOOL DFIsDeprecatedAddControl(UIControl *control) {
    if (![control isKindOfClass:UIControl.class]) return NO;
    // Match the registered action, never a localized label or generic "+".
    for (id target in control.allTargets) {
        NSArray<NSString *> *actions =
            [control actionsForTarget:target forControlEvent:UIControlEventTouchUpInside];
        if ([actions containsObject:@"addMoreAppsTapped:"]) return YES;
    }
    return NO;
}
static NSUInteger DFRemoveAddControls(UIView *view, NSUInteger depth) {
    if (!view || depth>20) return 0;
    NSUInteger count=0;
    // Take a snapshot because removeFromSuperview mutates subviews.
    for (UIView *child in [view.subviews copy]) {
        if ([child isKindOfClass:UIControl.class] &&
            DFIsDeprecatedAddControl((UIControl *)child)) {
            child.hidden=YES;
            [child removeFromSuperview];
            count++;
            continue;
        }
        count+=DFRemoveAddControls(child,depth+1);
    }
    return count;
}
static void DFHideLegacyAddButton(UIView *overlay) {
    if (![NSThread isMainThread]) return;
    NSUInteger removed=DFRemoveAddControls(overlay,0);
    if (removed) NSLog(@"[DuoFace] removed obsolete Add App tile (count=%lu)",
                       (unsigned long)removed);
}
%group DFRemoveOldAddAppTile
%hook CBMultitaskingOverlayView
- (void)setupAppPickerCardsInCardW:(CGFloat)w cardH:(CGFloat)h contentY:(CGFloat)y {
    %orig;
    DFHideLegacyAddButton(self);
}
- (void)layoutSubviews {
    %orig;
    DFHideLegacyAddButton(self);
}
- (void)addMoreAppsTapped:(id)sender {
    // A stale retained button/gesture must NEVER recreate the old dialog.
    (void)sender;
    NSLog(@"[DuoFace] ignored obsolete Add App action");
}
%end
%end

static BOOL DFRemoveTileHooked=NO;
static BOOL DFAllowedCarPlayHost(void) {
    NSString *bundle=NSBundle.mainBundle.bundleIdentifier.lowercaseString;
    NSString *process=NSProcessInfo.processInfo.processName.lowercaseString;
    return [@[@"com.apple.springboard",@"com.apple.carplay",
              @"com.apple.carplayapp",@"com.apple.dashboard"] containsObject:bundle] ||
           [@[@"springboard",@"carplay",@"carplayapp",@"dashboard"] containsObject:process];
}
static void DFInstallOldAddTileRemoval(void) {
    if (DFRemoveTileHooked || !DFAllowedCarPlayHost()) return;
    if (NSClassFromString(@"CBMultitaskingOverlayView")==Nil) {
        dispatch_after(dispatch_time(DISPATCH_TIME_NOW,5*NSEC_PER_SEC),
                       dispatch_get_main_queue(),^{DFInstallOldAddTileRemoval();});
        return;
    }
    %init(DFRemoveOldAddAppTile);
    DFRemoveTileHooked=YES;
    NSLog(@"[DuoFace] obsolete Add App removal hook installed");
}
__attribute__((constructor)) static void DFStartRemoveOldAddTile(void) {
    @autoreleasepool {
        if (!DFAllowedCarPlayHost()) return;
        dispatch_async(dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{DFInstallOldAddTileRemoval();});
    }
}
