########## BEGIN FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionAdapter.h ##########
#pragma once
// SpringBoard owner adapter interface; callbacks must be bound to verified
// native DuoFace methods only after their signature and process are audited.
// This file does not install hooks or change the user's existing settings.
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>
NS_ASSUME_NONNULL_BEGIN
typedef BOOL (^DFConnectionQuery)(void);
typedef BOOL (^DFAppQuery)(NSString *bundleID);
typedef void (^DFAppExecutor)(NSString *bundleID);
typedef void (^DFLastAppSaver)(NSString *bundleID);
FOUNDATION_EXPORT void DFConnectionConfigure(BOOL autoStart, BOOL closeOnDisconnect);
FOUNDATION_EXPORT void DFConnectionLoadRememberedApp(NSString * _Nullable existingPreferenceBundleID);
FOUNDATION_EXPORT void DFConnectionManagedAppActivated(NSString *bundleID,
                                                       DFLastAppSaver _Nullable saveLastApp);
FOUNDATION_EXPORT void DFConnectionVerifiedConnected(NSString * _Nullable currentDuoFaceOwnedApp,
                                                      DFAppQuery installed,
                                                      DFAppExecutor _Nullable launchApp,
                                                      DFLastAppSaver _Nullable saveLastApp);
FOUNDATION_EXPORT void DFConnectionVerifiedDisconnected(DFConnectionQuery stillDisconnected,
                                                         DFAppQuery stillOwnedAndRunning,
                                                         DFAppExecutor terminateOwnedApp);
NS_ASSUME_NONNULL_END
########## END FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionAdapter.h ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionAdapter.mm ##########
// No hook installed here. This adapter must be called from validated
// SpringBoard CBBridgeManager connect/disconnect and managed-app callbacks.
// Do not treat a Dashboard app deactivation as CarPlay disconnect.
// Existing user preferences and existing activation callbacks remain native.
#import "DFConnectionAdapter.h"
#import "DFConnectionPolicy.hpp"
#import <dispatch/dispatch.h>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

using duoface::connection::Policy;
using duoface::connection::Action;
using duoface::connection::ActionKind;

static Policy& DFPolicy() { static Policy p; return p; }
static void DFAssertOwnerThread() {
    NSCAssert([NSThread isMainThread],
              @"DuoFace connection adapter must run on owner process main thread");
}
static std::string DFUtf8(NSString *value) {
    return [value isKindOfClass:NSString.class] && value.UTF8String
        ? std::string(value.UTF8String) : std::string();
}
static NSString *DFString(const std::string& value) {
    return [NSString stringWithUTF8String:value.c_str()] ?: @"";
}
static std::uint64_t DFUptimeMilliseconds() {
    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(NSProcessInfo.processInfo.systemUptime*1000.0);
}
static void DFDispatchActions(const std::vector<Action>& actions,
                              DFAppExecutor launch,
                              DFAppExecutor terminate,
                              DFLastAppSaver save) {
    for (const auto& action : actions) {
        NSString *app=DFString(action.bundleId);
        if (!app.length) continue;
        switch (action.kind) {
            case ActionKind::LaunchApp:
                if (launch) launch(app);
                break;
            case ActionKind::TerminateOwnedApp:
                if (terminate) terminate(app);
                break;
            case ActionKind::SaveLastApp:
                if (save) save(app);
                break;
        }
    }
}
void DFConnectionConfigure(BOOL autoStart, BOOL closeOnDisconnect) {
    DFAssertOwnerThread();
    DFPolicy().setAutoStart(autoStart);
    DFPolicy().setCloseOnDisconnect(closeOnDisconnect);
}
void DFConnectionLoadRememberedApp(NSString *bundleID) {
    DFAssertOwnerThread();
    DFPolicy().restoreLastAppFromExistingPreferences(DFUtf8(bundleID));
}
void DFConnectionManagedAppActivated(NSString *bundleID, DFLastAppSaver saveLastApp) {
    DFAssertOwnerThread();
    DFDispatchActions(DFPolicy().onManagedAppActivated(DFUtf8(bundleID)),
                      nil,nil,saveLastApp);
}
void DFConnectionVerifiedConnected(NSString *currentDuoFaceOwnedApp,
                                   DFAppQuery installed, DFAppExecutor launchApp,
                                   DFLastAppSaver saveLastApp) {
    DFAssertOwnerThread();
    // Do NOT issue an app launch unless the real owner supplied a validated,
    // installed candidate, and currentApp has been checked for ownership.
    Policy::Predicate check=[installed](const std::string& bid) {
        return installed && installed(DFString(bid));
    };
    DFDispatchActions(
        DFPolicy().onVerifiedConnected(DFUtf8(currentDuoFaceOwnedApp),check),
        launchApp,nil,saveLastApp);
}
void DFConnectionVerifiedDisconnected(DFConnectionQuery stillDisconnected,
                                      DFAppQuery stillOwnedAndRunning,
                                      DFAppExecutor terminateOwnedApp) {
    DFAssertOwnerThread();
    auto& policy=DFPolicy();
    policy.onVerifiedDisconnected(DFUptimeMilliseconds());
    if (!policy.hasPendingClose()) return;
    const std::uint64_t token=policy.generation();
    // On reconnect policy generation changes, so old timers cannot terminate.
    // Delay exactly 10 seconds; the current preference can cancel at any time.
    dispatch_after(dispatch_time(DISPATCH_TIME_NOW,
                   static_cast<int64_t>(Policy::kDisconnectGraceMs)*NSEC_PER_MSEC),
                   dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
        DFAssertOwnerThread();
        if (DFPolicy().generation()!=token) return;
        const bool disconnected=stillDisconnected && stillDisconnected();
        Policy::Predicate canClose=[stillOwnedAndRunning](const std::string& bid) {
            // Both owned and running must be proved by the actual host.
            return stillOwnedAndRunning && stillOwnedAndRunning(DFString(bid));
        };
        auto actions=DFPolicy().tick(DFUptimeMilliseconds(), disconnected, canClose);
        DFDispatchActions(actions,nil,terminateOwnedApp,nil);
    });
}
########## END FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionAdapter.mm ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionNativeHooks.xm ##########
// DuoFace-only native12 SpringBoard connection bridge.
// native21: keep 10-second disconnect only; native12 owns Auto Resume.
// Preserve every original callback. No DuoDash code, logos, assets or UI.
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>
#import <objc/runtime.h>
#import "DFConnectionAdapter.h"

@interface CBLicenseSyncManager : NSObject
+ (BOOL)isCarPlayScreenConnected;  // native12 B16@0:8, byte-exact preserved
@end

@interface CBDashboardSettings : NSObject
+ (instancetype)sharedInstance;
- (BOOL)autoLaunchOnConnect;
@end

@interface CBBridgeManager : NSObject
+ (instancetype)sharedInstanceIfExists;
- (BOOL)activated;
- (NSString *)bundleIdentifier;
- (BOOL)isCurrentlyBridging:(NSString *)bundleID;
- (NSArray *)runningBridgedApplications;
- (NSString *)effectiveAutoLaunchBundleID;
- (void)terminateApplicationWithBundleID:(NSString *)bundleID;
@end

static NSString *const kDFSmartPrefs = @"com.duoface.smartconnection";
static NSString *const kDFClose = @"closeOnCarPlayDisconnect";
static BOOL DFConnectedKnown = NO;
static NSUInteger DFConnectionEpoch = 0;

static NSUserDefaults *DFStore(void) {
    return [[NSUserDefaults alloc] initWithSuiteName:kDFSmartPrefs];
}
static BOOL DFHostCarPlayConnected(void) {
    Class cls=NSClassFromString(@"CBLicenseSyncManager");
    if (!cls || ![cls respondsToSelector:@selector(isCarPlayScreenConnected)])
        return NO; // Fail closed when host cannot prove a real CarPlay screen.
    return [cls isCarPlayScreenConnected];
}
static BOOL DFOwnedAndRunning(CBBridgeManager *manager, NSString *bundleID) {
    if (![bundleID isKindOfClass:NSString.class] || !bundleID.length) return NO;
    if ([manager isCurrentlyBridging:bundleID]) return YES;
    NSArray *running = [manager runningBridgedApplications];
    return [running isKindOfClass:NSArray.class] && [running containsObject:bundleID];
}
static BOOL DFAutoEnabled(void) {
    Class cls=NSClassFromString(@"CBDashboardSettings");
    if (!cls || ![cls respondsToSelector:@selector(sharedInstance)]) return NO;
    CBDashboardSettings *settings=[cls sharedInstance];
    return settings && [settings respondsToSelector:@selector(autoLaunchOnConnect)]
        && settings.autoLaunchOnConnect;
}
static void DFRefreshConnectionState(CBBridgeManager *manager) {
    if (![NSThread isMainThread] || !manager) return;
    // The old CarPlay detection implementation is observed, never swizzled.
    Class detector=NSClassFromString(@"CBLicenseSyncManager");
    if (!detector || ![detector respondsToSelector:@selector(isCarPlayScreenConnected)])
        return; // Without a verified event owner, do nothing.
    BOOL connected=DFHostCarPlayConnected();
    DFConnectionConfigure(DFAutoEnabled(),[DFStore() boolForKey:kDFClose]);
    if (connected==DFConnectedKnown) return;
    DFConnectedKnown=connected;
    ++DFConnectionEpoch;
    if (connected) {
        // The native CBBridgeManager's own performAutoResume does the launch;
        // deliberately pass NO launch executor to prevent a second launch.
        NSString *app=([manager activated] &&
                        DFOwnedAndRunning(manager,[manager bundleIdentifier]))
                      ? [manager bundleIdentifier] : nil;
        DFConnectionLoadRememberedApp(nil); // native12 owns Auto Resume preferences
        DFConnectionVerifiedConnected(app,
            ^BOOL(NSString *bid){return DFOwnedAndRunning(manager,bid);},
            nil, ^(NSString *bid){ (void)bid; }); // do not persist single-app recall
    } else {
        DFConnectionVerifiedDisconnected(
            ^BOOL {
                // Re-read cross-process preference at grace expiry.
                DFConnectionConfigure(DFAutoEnabled(),[DFStore() boolForKey:kDFClose]);
                return !DFHostCarPlayConnected();
            },
            ^BOOL(NSString *bid){return DFOwnedAndRunning(manager,bid);},
            ^(NSString *bid){
                if (!DFHostCarPlayConnected() && DFOwnedAndRunning(manager,bid))
                    [manager terminateApplicationWithBundleID:bid];
            });
    }
}
static void DFObserveNativeConnection(CBBridgeManager *manager) {
    if ([NSThread isMainThread]) { DFRefreshConnectionState(manager); return; }
    dispatch_async(dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
        DFRefreshConnectionState(manager);
    });
}

// These exact native12 classes and ABIs were audited in GitHub Actions.
// All callbacks call %orig. Only DuoFace-owned app IDs are recorded.
%hook CBBridgeManager
- (void)handleCarPlayConnectionChanged:(id)notification {
    %orig;
    DFObserveNativeConnection(self);
}
// The immutable native12 contains its own unconditional mandatory watchdog.
// While the NEW close-on-disconnect option is ON, our scoped 10-second timer
// must be the only shutdown authority. Otherwise the original watchdog could
// forcibly kill apps before the user-selected grace period or kill twice.
// OFF deliberately preserves existing native12 behavior; no global override.
- (void)scheduleMandatoryDisconnectWatchdog {
    if ([DFStore() boolForKey:kDFClose]) {
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceSmartConnection] prevent parallel native watchdog; managed 10s timer owns close");
        return;
    }
    %orig;
}
- (void)enforceMandatoryDisconnectKill {
    if ([DFStore() boolForKey:kDFClose]) {
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceSmartConnection] ignore stale native mandatory kill; scoped 10s policy active");
        return;
    }
    %orig;
}
- (void)handleScreenDidConnect:(id)notification {
    %orig;
    DFObserveNativeConnection(self);
}
- (void)handleScreenDidDisconnect:(id)notification {
    %orig;
    DFObserveNativeConnection(self);
}
- (void)trackBridgedBundleID:(id)rawBundleID {
    %orig;
    if (![rawBundleID isKindOfClass:NSString.class]) return;
    NSString *bundleID=(NSString *)rawBundleID;
    CBBridgeManager *manager=self;
    dispatch_async(dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
        if (!DFHostCarPlayConnected() || !DFOwnedAndRunning(manager,bundleID)) return;
        DFRefreshConnectionState(manager);
        DFConnectionManagedAppActivated(bundleID, nil);
    });
}
%end

__attribute__((constructor)) static void DFSmartConnectionBoot(void) {
    @autoreleasepool {
        NSString *bundle=NSBundle.mainBundle.bundleIdentifier.lowercaseString;
        if (![bundle isEqualToString:@"com.apple.springboard"]) return;
        // Hooks are only compiled in the isolated candidate dylib; do not
        // change native12 or touch CarPlay geometry/UI at constructor time.
        NSLog(@"[DuoFaceSmartConnection] source bridge loaded in SpringBoard");
    }
}
########## END FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionNativeHooks.xm ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionPolicy.cpp ##########
#include "DFConnectionPolicy.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <limits>
#include <utility>

namespace duoface { namespace connection {
bool Policy::validBundleId(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.size() < 3 || s.size() > 255 || s.front() == '.' || s.back() == '.'
        || s.find('.') == std::string::npos || s.find("..") != std::string::npos)
        return false;
    for (unsigned char c : s) {
        if (!(std::isalnum(c) || c == '.' || c == '-' || c == '_'))
            return false;
    }
    return true;
}
void Policy::clearPendingClose() {
    pendingClose_ = false;
    dueAtMs_ = 0;
    pendingOwnedApps_.clear();
}
void Policy::setAutoStart(bool enabled) {
    autoStart_ = enabled;
    if (!enabled) pendingLaunch_.clear();
}
void Policy::setCloseOnDisconnect(bool enabled) {
    closeOnDisconnect_ = enabled;
    if (!enabled) clearPendingClose();  // user switches OFF during grace
}
void Policy::restoreLastAppFromExistingPreferences(const std::string& id) {
    // Preference adapter owns the domain; never overwrite the old key here.
    lastApp_ = validBundleId(id) ? id : std::string();
}
std::vector<Action> Policy::onManagedAppActivated(const std::string& id) {
    if (!connected_ || !validBundleId(id)) return {};
    sessionOwnedApps_.insert(id);
    pendingLaunch_.clear();
    if (lastApp_ == id) return {};
    lastApp_ = id;
    return {{ActionKind::SaveLastApp, lastApp_, generation_}};
}
std::vector<Action> Policy::onVerifiedConnected(const std::string& activeOwnedApp,
                                                 const Predicate& installed) {
    if (connected_) return {};  // duplicate event: never launch twice
    connected_ = true;
    ++generation_;             // invalidate all stale disconnect callbacks
    auto previouslyOwned = pendingOwnedApps_;
    clearPendingClose();
    pendingLaunch_.clear();
    sessionOwnedApps_.clear();
    if (validBundleId(activeOwnedApp)) {
        sessionOwnedApps_.insert(activeOwnedApp);
        // The active app is already on the display; no new launch.
        return onManagedAppActivated(activeOwnedApp);
    }
    // A pending app from before a short disconnect is not proof of a currently
    // running app, and does not establish ownership for the *new* session.
    (void)previouslyOwned;
    if (!autoStart_ || !validBundleId(lastApp_) || !installed || !installed(lastApp_))
        return {};
    pendingLaunch_ = lastApp_;
    return {{ActionKind::LaunchApp, lastApp_, generation_}};
}
void Policy::onVerifiedDisconnected(std::uint64_t now) {
    if (!connected_) return;
    connected_ = false;
    ++generation_;
    pendingLaunch_.clear();
    if (!closeOnDisconnect_ || sessionOwnedApps_.empty()) {
        clearPendingClose();
        sessionOwnedApps_.clear();
        return;
    }
    pendingOwnedApps_ = sessionOwnedApps_;
    sessionOwnedApps_.clear();
    // Saturate rather than let a wrapped tick appear immediately due.
    dueAtMs_ = now > std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max()-kDisconnectGraceMs
        ? std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max() : now+kDisconnectGraceMs;
    pendingClose_ = true;
}
std::vector<Action> Policy::tick(std::uint64_t now, bool confirmedDisconnected,
                                 const Predicate& canTerminate) {
    if (connected_ || !pendingClose_ || now < dueAtMs_) return {};
    auto candidates = pendingOwnedApps_;
    clearPendingClose(); // at most once, even if callback retries
    if (!closeOnDisconnect_ || !confirmedDisconnected || !canTerminate)
        return {};
    std::vector<Action> result;
    for (const auto& id : candidates) {
        if (validBundleId(id) && canTerminate(id))
            result.push_back({ActionKind::TerminateOwnedApp,id,generation_});
    }
    return result;
}
}} // namespace duoface::connection
########## END FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionPolicy.cpp ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionSettingsHook.xm ##########
// Source-staged iPhone DuoFaceUI Settings extension; not deployed.
// Extends the original native12 CBGeneralSettingsViewController table by
// ONE section/row. Preserves its old Auto Start switch and all section indexes.
#import <UIKit/UIKit.h>
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>

static NSString *const kDFSettingsSuite = @"com.duoface.smartconnection";
static NSString *const kDFDisconnectCloseKey = @"closeOnCarPlayDisconnect";

static NSUInteger DFNativeSectionCount(id controller, UITableView *tableView) {
    // Call the hooked numberOfSections method, subtract our single appended
    // section. No hardcoded index: original sections can change with language.
    NSInteger all = (NSInteger)[controller numberOfSectionsInTableView:tableView];
    return (NSUInteger)(all>0 ? all-1 : 0);
}
static NSUserDefaults *DFConnectionSettingsStore(void) {
    return [[NSUserDefaults alloc] initWithSuiteName:kDFSettingsSuite];
}
%hook CBGeneralSettingsViewController
- (NSInteger)numberOfSectionsInTableView:(UITableView *)tableView {
    NSInteger native=%orig;
    return native>=0 ? native+1 : native;
}
- (NSInteger)tableView:(UITableView *)tableView numberOfRowsInSection:(NSInteger)section {
    if ((NSUInteger)section==DFNativeSectionCount(self,tableView)) return 1;
    return %orig;
}
- (NSString *)tableView:(UITableView *)tableView titleForHeaderInSection:(NSInteger)section {
    if ((NSUInteger)section==DFNativeSectionCount(self,tableView))
        return @"KẾT NỐI CARPLAY";
    return %orig;
}
- (UITableViewCell *)tableView:(UITableView *)tableView
          cellForRowAtIndexPath:(NSIndexPath *)indexPath {
    if ((NSUInteger)indexPath.section!=DFNativeSectionCount(self,tableView))
        return %orig;
    static NSString *identifier=@"DFCloseOnDisconnectCell";
    UITableViewCell *cell=[tableView dequeueReusableCellWithIdentifier:identifier];
    if (!cell) cell=[[UITableViewCell alloc] initWithStyle:UITableViewCellStyleSubtitle
                                           reuseIdentifier:identifier];
    cell.textLabel.text=@"Đóng ứng dụng khi ngắt CarPlay";
    cell.detailTextLabel.text=@"Chờ 10 giây; kết nối lại sẽ hủy";
    cell.selectionStyle=UITableViewCellSelectionStyleNone;
    UISwitch *toggle=[UISwitch new];
    toggle.on=[DFConnectionSettingsStore() boolForKey:kDFDisconnectCloseKey];
    [toggle addTarget:self action:@selector(dfCloseOnDisconnectChanged:)
     forControlEvents:UIControlEventValueChanged];
    cell.accessoryView=toggle;
    return cell;
}
- (void)tableView:(UITableView *)tableView didSelectRowAtIndexPath:(NSIndexPath *)indexPath {
    if ((NSUInteger)indexPath.section==DFNativeSectionCount(self,tableView)) return;
    %orig;
}
%new
- (void)dfCloseOnDisconnectChanged:(UISwitch *)sender {
    if (![sender isKindOfClass:UISwitch.class]) return;
    NSUserDefaults *prefs=DFConnectionSettingsStore();
    [prefs setBool:sender.on forKey:kDFDisconnectCloseKey];
    // Other process reads suite value at event time and after the grace
    // period; no command to force close or respring is sent from the UI.
}
%end
########## END FILE: runtime/connection/DFConnectionSettingsHook.xm ##########
